# Provider Catalog sources

## Botscanner (AutoBench Router) — 2026-11-11

`botscanner` / `Botscanner (AutoBench Router)` is a gateway Provider for the Botscanner Router, a service of AutoBench, a project of eZecute S.R.L. The [terms](https://botscanner.ai/terms-of-service) name that company at Via Ludovico di Savoia, 2b, 00185 Rome, Italy, REA di Roma Nº 1302638, P.IVA 11435051005. The [FAQ](https://botscanner.ai/faq) and the [homepage](https://botscanner.ai/) set the OpenAI client `base_url` to `https://api.autobench.org/v1` and show `client.chat.completions.create`. The example key is `ab_live_YOUR_KEY`. The example model is `autobench/beta-balanced`. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on `https://api.autobench.org/v1` and sends `Authorization: Bearer`. The [OpenAPI](https://api.autobench.org/openapi.json) document (`Autobench Router API` 0.1.0) lists `POST /v1/chat/completions` and `GET /v1/models`. It does not list `/v1/responses`, `/v1/messages`, or a Gemini route, so none is stored.

Probes on 2026-11-11: unsigned `GET https://api.autobench.org/v1/models` returned HTTP 401 `{"error":{"message":"invalid api key","type":"invalid_api_key","code":"invalid_api_key"}}`. The body was not `{"object":"list","data":...}`. A bogus Bearer key on that GET returned the same HTTP 401. Unsigned `POST /v1/chat/completions` with `autobench/beta-cost` returned the same HTTP 401. A bogus Bearer key on that POST returned the same HTTP 401. A bogus `x-api-key` on that POST returned the same HTTP 401, so this probe does not separate the header name. The FAQ's OpenAI client is what documents Bearer. Unsigned `POST /v1/responses`, a bogus Bearer key on that route, and unsigned `POST /responses` returned HTTP 404 `{"detail":"Not Found"}`. Unsigned `POST /v1/messages` with `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01`, the same route with a bogus Bearer key and that version header, the same route with a bogus `x-api-key` and that version header, and unsigned `POST /messages` returned HTTP 404 `{"detail":"Not Found"}`. Unsigned `POST /v1beta/models/autobench/beta-cost:generateContent` and a bogus Bearer key on that route returned HTTP 404 `{"detail":"Not Found"}`. Unsigned `GET /v1/embeddings`, `POST /v1/embeddings`, and `POST /v1/completions` returned HTTP 404 `{"detail":"Not Found"}`. `GET /`, `GET /v1`, and `GET /v1/not-a-route` returned HTTP 404 `{"detail":"Not Found"}`. `GET /v1/chat/completions` returned HTTP 405 `{"detail":"Method Not Allowed"}`. Unsigned `GET /v1/health` returned HTTP 200 `{"ok":true,"models_loaded":{"b10a":true,"b11a":true,"brick":true}}`. Unsigned `GET /v1/usage` returned the same HTTP 401 as `/v1/models`.

Anonymous `GET /v1/models` is not an OpenAI catalog. The OpenAPI lists the route, and both an unsigned request and a bogus Bearer key returned HTTP 401. The authenticated 200 body was not observed. No reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered, and this id is not in `EXPECTED_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE`. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is `autobench/beta-cost`, `autobench/beta-balanced`, and `autobench/beta-quality`. The FAQ names those three knobs and says a chat request is rejected if it asks for a specific model instead of a knob. The [Models](https://botscanner.ai/models) page lists playground ids such as `qwen/qwen3.7-flash`, `openai/gpt-6-luna`, `anthropic/claude-opus-5.5`, and `google/gemini-4-argon`. Those ids are not seeded. The seed stores no display name and no context length. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

The FAQ says new accounts receive $1 in free credits and that no credit card is required to start. Signup asks for an invitation code. The [about](https://botscanner.ai/about) page says to sign up with an invitation code or request one. Free accounts start on the Cost knob. Quality and Balanced unlock at Tier 1, once credit purchases add up to $10. The FAQ and the terms say the Router chat and the Router API price is the upstream model's billed cost plus a markup, currently 50%. Selecting the route is not charged. The Models page labels every listed model's API Type as `openrouter`. The catalog does not encode the credit, the tier, or the markup. The template portal is <https://botscanner.ai/api-keys> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned 200 (title `Botscanner - Login`). Unsigned GET of the FAQ returned 200 (title `Botscanner - Frequently Asked Questions | Botscanner Router`). Unsigned GET of the homepage returned 200 (title `Botscanner - State-of-the-Art AI at a Fraction of the Cost`).

Host inference maps `api.autobench.org` for every path, including `/v1`, `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/models`, `/v1/responses`, `/v1/messages`, `/openapi.json`, and the origin. `botscanner.ai` is the FAQ, the homepage, the about page, the Models page, and the key app. `www.botscanner.ai` returned HTTP 200 and the same homepage title; it did not redirect. `botscanner.autobench.org` returned HTTP 200 and the same welcome page. `autobench.org` and `www.autobench.org` returned HTTP 200 (title `Home - AutoBench`). Those hosts stay `custom`. `api.botscanner.ai`, `www.api.autobench.org`, and `api.autobench.com` did not resolve. Those stay `custom`, as do lookalikes.

## 共绩算力 MaaS — 2026-11-11

`suanli-maas` / `共绩算力 MaaS` is a gateway Provider for 共绩算力 MaaS, operated by 北京共绩科技有限公司. The site footer states that name, with 京ICP备2023013220号-1. [gongjiyun.com](https://gongjiyun.com/), `www.gongjiyun.com`, and `http://gongjiyun.com/` returned HTTP 302 to `https://suanli.cn/`. The [quickstart](https://www.suanli.cn/docs/large-model-cloud-service/quickstart/cqsrwzrbsim4idkkjbxcdux4nfg/) sets the OpenAI SDK `base_url` to `https://api.suanli.cn/v1` and documents `POST https://api.suanli.cn/v1/chat/completions` with `Authorization: Bearer`. The example model is `deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash`. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on `https://api.suanli.cn/v1`. The same page documents authenticated `models.list()` on that base. No Responses, Anthropic Messages, or Gemini contract is documented, so none is stored.

Probes on 2026-11-11: unsigned `GET https://api.suanli.cn/v1/models` returned HTTP 400 `{"code":400,"message":"缺少用户凭证"}`. The body was not `{"object":"list","data":...}`. A bogus Bearer key on that GET returned HTTP 401 `{"error":{"code":"invalid_api_key","message":"当前 API Key 不存在","type":"invalid_api_key"}}`. Unsigned `POST /v1/chat/completions` with `deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash` returned the same HTTP 400. A bogus Bearer key on that POST returned the same HTTP 401, so Bearer is the credential the route reads. Unsigned `POST /v1/responses`, `POST /responses`, `POST /v1/messages` with `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01`, `POST /messages` with that header, and `POST /v1beta/models/deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash:generateContent` returned the same HTTP 400. A bogus Bearer key on `/v1/responses`, `/v1/messages`, the Gemini route, `/v1/embeddings`, `/v1/completions`, and `GET /v1/not-a-route` returned the same HTTP 401. That 401 is the credential gate, including on a route that does not exist, so those probes do not establish Responses, Messages, or Gemini. Unsigned `GET /`, `GET /v1`, `GET /v1/chat/completions`, and `GET /v1/not-a-route` returned the same HTTP 400. `GET https://suanli.cn/v1/models` and `GET https://www.suanli.cn/v1/models` returned openresty HTTP 404.

Anonymous `GET /v1/models` is not an OpenAI catalog. The quickstart says the model list requires an API key. No reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered, and this id is not in `EXPECTED_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE`. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is `deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash`, `deepseek/deepseek-v4-pro`, `moonshotai/kimi-k3`, `z-ai/glm-5.2`, and `qwen/qwen3.7-max`. The [product page](https://suanli.cn/product/token/) prints those ids, plus `moonshotai/kimi-k2.6`, `z-ai/glm-5`, `z-ai/glm-5.1`, and `tencent/hy3-preview`. Those four are not seeded. The seed stores no display name and no context length. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

The product page and the [pricing page](https://suanli.cn/price/) say token billing is pay-as-you-go, with no subscription fee and no time limit. The pricing table publishes input, output, and cache prices for Kimi K3, GLM 5.2, DeepSeek V4 Pro, DeepSeek V4 Flash, Kimi K2.6, Qwen 3.7 Max, and Tencent/hy3-preview. The product navigation includes 免费注册. That label is account signup. Those pages do not publish a standing free token pool. The catalog does not encode a price. The template portal is <https://console.suanli.cn/models> with kind `api-key`. The quickstart links that URL for the Access Token. Its environment table calls the same URL the console for the model square, playground, key management, and billing. An unsigned GET returned 200 (Next.js shell, no document title). `GET https://console.suanli.cn/api-keys` returned HTTP 404. Unsigned GET of the quickstart returned 200 (title `快速上手大模型云服务 | 共绩算力`). The same path without a trailing slash on `suanli.cn` returned HTTP 301 to `http://suanli.cn/docs/large-model-cloud-service/quickstart/cqsrwzrbsim4idkkjbxcdux4nfg/`. The stored docs URL keeps the trailing slash. Unsigned GET of the product page returned 200 (title `大模型云服务_Token_API服务_OpenAI兼容接口_模型聚合 | 共绩算力`). Unsigned GET of the pricing page returned 200 (title `算力及存储定价 | 共绩算力`).

Host inference maps `api.suanli.cn` for every path, including `/v1`, `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/models`, `/v1/responses`, `/v1/messages`, and the origin. `suanli.cn` and `www.suanli.cn` are the site, the quickstart, the product page, and pricing. `www.suanli.cn` returned HTTP 200 and did not redirect; its canonical URL is `https://suanli.cn/`. `console.suanli.cn` is the key app. `gongjiyun.com` redirects to the site. `api.gongjiyun.com` returned HTTP 302 to `https://suanli.cn/v1/models`. Those hosts stay `custom`. `www.api.suanli.cn` failed TLS hostname verification. `api.suanli.com` returned HTTP 404 `{"error":{"message":"not found","type":"invalid_request_error"}}`. Those stay `custom`, as do lookalikes.

## Free.ai — 2026-11-11

`free-ai` / `Free.ai` is a gateway Provider for the [Free.ai API](https://free.ai/api/). That page and the [quickstart](https://free.ai/docs/) document `POST https://api.free.ai/v1/chat/` with `Authorization: Bearer`. Keys look like `sk-free-...`. The quickstart says an OpenAI SDK sets `base_url` to `https://api.free.ai/v1`. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on that base, so the request is `POST /v1/chat/completions`. The documented `/v1/chat/` route is the path those pages curl. No Responses or Gemini contract is documented, so neither is stored. [llms.txt](https://free.ai/llms.txt) says the API is compatible with Anthropic Messages. That claim is not stored: `POST /v1/messages` returned HTTP 404.

Probes on 2026-11-11: unsigned `GET https://api.free.ai/v1/models` returned HTTP 200. The JSON keys were `models`, `total`, and `caller_country`, with `total` 481. The body was not `{"object":"list","data":...}`. A bogus Bearer key on that GET returned the same HTTP 200. Unsigned `POST /v1/chat/completions` with `qwen7b` returned HTTP 200 `chat.completion` and `"model":"Qwen/Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct"`. A bogus Bearer key on that POST returned HTTP 200 with the same `model`. Unsigned `POST /v1/chat/` with `qwen7b` returned HTTP 200 and the same `model`. `POST /v1/chat` returned HTTP 307 to `/v1/chat/`. Unsigned `POST /v1/chat/completions` with `qwen-coder` returned HTTP 200 and the same `model`. Unsigned `POST /v1/chat/completions` with `openai/gpt-4o-mini` returned HTTP 400 saying that id is not served on this endpoint and that premium external models run on `POST /v1/chat/`. Unsigned `POST /v1/chat/` with `openai/gpt-4o-mini` returned HTTP 429 `premium_requires_purchase`. Unsigned `POST /v1/responses`, a bogus Bearer key on that route, and unsigned `POST /responses` returned HTTP 404 `{"detail":"Not Found"}`. Unsigned `POST /v1/messages` with `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01`, the same route with a bogus `x-api-key` and that version header, the same route with a bogus Bearer key and that version header, and unsigned `POST /messages` returned HTTP 404 `{"detail":"Not Found"}`. Unsigned `POST /v1beta/models/qwen7b:generateContent` returned HTTP 404 `{"detail":"Not Found"}`. `GET /v1/not-a-route` and `GET /` returned HTTP 404 `{"detail":"Not Found"}`. `GET /v1/chat/completions` returned HTTP 405 `{"detail":"Method Not Allowed"}`.

Anonymous `GET /v1/models` is not an OpenAI catalog. The list mixes chat with image, video, speech, OCR, and other rows. No reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered, and this id is not in `EXPECTED_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE`. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is `qwen7b` and `qwen-coder`. The catalog names both `Qwen 3.6 35B`, type `chat` and `code`, `self_hosted` true, prompt and completion price `0`. The same display name is on `qwen3-coder`, `mistral`, and `qwen3-8b`. `deepseek-r1` and `deepseek-r1-7b` are named `Qwen 3.6 35B Thinking`. `qwen-vl` and `qwen25-vl` are named `Qwen 3.6 35B` with type vision. Those ids are not seeded. Completions for `qwen7b` and `qwen-coder` returned `model` `Qwen/Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct`. That upstream id is not a catalog row and is not seeded. The seed stores no display name and no context length. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

The [pricing FAQ](https://free.ai/pricing/) says confirmed accounts get 30,000 free tokens per day that reset every 24 hours. It publishes no end date. The [about](https://free.ai/about/) page says 10,000 tokens on signup plus 30,000 renewable tokens daily, and no credit card. The [terms](https://free.ai/terms/) say Free.ai is operated by Muddy Holdings LLC, that the daily allowance replenishes at midnight UTC, and that the allowance is best-effort and may change without notice. They publish no end date. The API page Free row is 30,000 tokens a day, 1,000 API requests a month, and 60 requests a minute, at $0. The quickstart FAQ says the free plan's monthly call cap is 1,000 and that there is no per-second throttle. The catalog does not encode the pool, the caps, or a price. The template portal is <https://free.ai/account/?tab=api> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned HTTP 302 to `/login/`. `GET https://free.ai/login/` returned 200 (title `Log in | Free.ai`). The quickstart says to sign up at <https://free.ai/signup/>, confirm the email, open the account API tab, and click Generate. An unsigned GET of that signup page returned 200 (title `Sign Up Free | Free.ai`). The page says 30,000 free tokens a day and no credit card. Unsigned GET of the API page returned 200 (title `Free AI API: Chat, Image, Video and Speech Endpoints | Free.ai`). Unsigned GET of the quickstart returned 200 (title `Docs - Quickstart in 60 Seconds | Free.ai`).

Host inference maps `api.free.ai` for every path, including `/v1`, `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/chat/`, `/v1/models`, `/v1/responses`, `/v1/messages`, and the origin. `free.ai` is the API reference, the quickstart, signup, and the key app. `GET https://free.ai/v1/models` returned HTTP 302 to `https://api.free.ai/v1/models`. That host stays `custom`. `www.free.ai` returned HTTP 301 to `http://free.ai/api/`. `www.api.free.ai` and `api.free.com` did not resolve. Those stay `custom`, as do lookalikes.

## cheapllm.shop — 2026-11-11

`cheapllm-shop` / `cheapllm.shop` is a gateway Provider for the [cheapllm.shop docs](https://cheapllm.shop/docs). That page sets the OpenAI SDK base to `https://cheapllm.shop/v1` and documents `POST https://cheapllm.shop/v1/chat/completions` with `Authorization: Bearer`. Keys look like `sk-cheap-...`. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on `https://cheapllm.shop/v1`. The same page says the `x-api-key` header works too. Arca sends Bearer on Chat Completions.

The docs' Anthropic and Responses section says tools built for the Anthropic Messages API point at `https://cheapllm.shop` and call `POST /v1/messages`. A base of `https://cheapllm.shop/v1` would request `/v1/v1/messages`. Arca stores Anthropic Messages at `https://cheapllm.shop`. The curl uses `x-api-key` and `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01`, which is the header Arca sends. That section also accepts `Authorization: Bearer`. The same section says tools built for the OpenAI Responses API call `POST /v1/responses` on `https://cheapllm.shop/v1`. Arca stores Responses at `https://cheapllm.shop/v1`. No Gemini contract is documented, so Gemini is not stored.

The three stored bases share the origin `https://cheapllm.shop`, so the stored paths are `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/responses`, and `/v1/messages`.

Probes on 2026-11-11: unsigned `GET https://cheapllm.shop/v1/models` returned HTTP 200 `{"object":"list","data":[{"id":"deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017","object":"model","created":1792195200,"owned_by":"cheapllm"}]}`. A bogus Bearer key on that GET returned HTTP 401 `invalid_api_key`. Unsigned `POST /v1/chat/completions` returned HTTP 401 `No API key. Send it as: Authorization: Bearer sk-cheap-...`. A bogus Bearer key on Chat Completions returned HTTP 401 `This API key is not valid. Grab a fresh one from your dashboard.` Unsigned `POST /v1/responses` and a bogus Bearer key on that route returned HTTP 401 `This API key is missing, not valid or revoked.` Unsigned `POST /responses` returned HTTP 405 `Method not allowed.` Unsigned `POST /v1/messages` with `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01` returned HTTP 401 in Anthropic's error shape: `No API key. Send it as x-api-key or Authorization: Bearer.` A bogus `x-api-key` and a bogus Bearer key on that route returned HTTP 401 `This API key is not valid or was revoked.`, so Arca's Messages header is accepted as a credential. Unsigned `POST /v1beta/models/deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017:generateContent` returned HTTP 405 `Method not allowed.` `GET /v1/not-a-route` returned HTTP 404 listing only `GET /v1/models`, `POST /v1/chat/completions`, `POST /v1/responses`, and `POST /v1/messages`.

Anonymous `GET /v1/models` is an OpenAI list with one chat row. Model discovery uses the OpenAI catalog decoder with an optional credential. With no credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is `deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017`. The docs say model names containing `deepseek` are routed to that id. Those aliases are not separate catalog rows and are not seeded. The seed stores no display name and no context length. A saved key loads `GET /v1/models`. This id is in `EXPECTED_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE`.

The docs say that during the beta every account is free, with no rate limit and no card. They publish no beta end date. When the beta ends, every account keeps a permanent free tier with no card: at least 5,000 input tokens and 1,000 output tokens per hour, counted per account over a rolling hour, all keys together. The page says those numbers can go up and never fall below that minimum. The catalog does not encode the beta, the floor, or a price. The template portal is <https://cheapllm.shop/signup> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned 200 (title `Get on the list / cheapllm.shop`). That page says the first API key is handed over on the next screen, with no card. Unsigned `GET /dashboard` returned HTTP 302 to `/login?next=%2Fdashboard`. The docs say later keys are created in the dashboard.

Host inference maps `cheapllm.shop` for every path, including `/v1`, `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/models`, `/v1/responses`, `/v1/messages`, `/docs`, `/signup`, `/login`, and `/dashboard`. The docs, signup, and dashboard share that host, so those paths infer to `cheapllm-shop`. `www.cheapllm.shop` and `api.cheapllm.shop` returned HTTP 404. Those stay `custom`, as do lookalikes.

## PowerTokens — 2026-11-11

`powertokens` / `PowerTokens` is a gateway Provider for [Create an API key and call a model](https://docs.powertokens.ai/en/guides/api-key-and-model-call). That page documents `POST https://api.powertokens.ai/v1/chat/completions` with `Authorization: Bearer`. The OpenAI SDK example sets `base_url` to `https://api.powertokens.ai/v1`. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on that base. The unlabeled Base URL box on that page is the origin `https://api.powertokens.ai`.

[Text model protocols and endpoints](https://docs.powertokens.ai/en/ecosystem-tools/text-model-protocols) documents `POST /v1/messages` and `POST /v1/responses` beside Chat Completions. The [Claude Code guide](https://docs.powertokens.ai/en/ecosystem-tools/claude-code) sets `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` to `https://api.powertokens.ai` and curls `POST https://api.powertokens.ai/v1/messages` with `Authorization: Bearer` and `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01`. A base of `https://api.powertokens.ai/v1` would request `/v1/v1/messages`. Arca stores Anthropic Messages at `https://api.powertokens.ai`. The Messages header Arca sends is `x-api-key` plus `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01`. Responses is stored at `https://api.powertokens.ai/v1`, so the client appends `/responses` and the request is `POST /v1/responses`. No Gemini contract is documented, so Gemini is not stored.

The three stored bases share the origin `https://api.powertokens.ai`, so the stored paths are `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/responses`, and `/v1/messages`.

Probes on 2026-11-11: unsigned `GET https://api.powertokens.ai/v1/models` returned HTTP 401 `token_invalid` with message `No token provided`. A bogus Bearer key on that GET returned HTTP 401 `Invalid token`. Unsigned `POST /v1/chat/completions`, `POST /v1/responses`, and `POST /v1/messages` returned that same no-token 401. A bogus Bearer key on Chat Completions and Responses returned `Invalid token`. A bogus `x-api-key` with `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01` on Messages returned that same `Invalid token`, so Arca's Messages header is accepted as a credential. Unsigned `POST /responses` and `POST /messages` returned nginx HTTP 404. Unsigned `POST /v1beta/models/glm-4.7-flash:generateContent` returned HTTP 404 `Invalid URL (POST /v1beta/models/glm-4.7-flash:generateContent)`. `GET /v1/not-a-route` returned HTTP 404 `Invalid URL (GET /v1/not-a-route)`. `GET /` returned nginx HTTP 404.

Anonymous `GET /v1/models` is not a catalog. The [opencode guide](https://docs.powertokens.ai/en/ecosystem-tools/opencode) says `/v1/models` also lists image, speech, and video rows. No reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered, and this id is not in `EXPECTED_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE`. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is `glm-4.7-flash`. The quickstart example `MiniMax-M3` is not seeded. The protocol matrix marks `glm-4.7-flash` Available on Chat Completions, Recommended on Anthropic Messages, and Not supported on Responses. The seed is the Chat Completions id. Responses stays a stored protocol for models the matrix marks Available. The seed stores no display name and no context length. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

The [GLM-4.7 Flash model page](https://powertokens.ai/models/glm-4.7-flash) shows Input $0 / 1M tokens and Output $0 / 1M tokens. It publishes no end date and no free-tier limit. The catalog does not encode that price. The template portal is <https://powertokens.ai/api-keys> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned 200 (title `API Keys | PowerTokens | Unified AI API for DeepSeek, Qwen, Seedance GLM and More`). `https://powertokens.ai/en/api-keys` returned HTTP 307 to `/api-keys`. The guide links <https://www.powertokens.ai/en/api-keys>, which returned HTTP 301 to `https://powertokens.ai/en/api-keys`.

Host inference maps `api.powertokens.ai` for every path, including `/v1`, `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/models`, `/v1/responses`, `/v1/messages`, and the origin. `docs.powertokens.ai` is the documentation site. `powertokens.ai` is the key app and the model page. Those stay `custom`, as do `www.powertokens.ai` and lookalikes. `www.api.powertokens.ai` did not resolve.

## Template corrections — 2026-11-10

Re-checked the provider templates below against the cited official pages on 2026-11-10. Provider ids are unchanged. Where a host moved, the previous host stays in `infer_provider`.

- `aihubmix` Gemini generateContent base is `https://aihubmix.com/gemini/v1beta`. The [Gemini SDK guide](https://docs.aihubmix.com/en/api/Gemini-SDK) sets the SDK base to `https://aihubmix.com/gemini`, and the Google client appends `/v1beta/models/...:generateContent`.
- `featherless-ai` portal is <https://featherless.ai/account/api-keys>. The [quickstart](https://featherless.ai/docs/quickstart-guide) links that page; unsigned requests redirect to `/login?returnTo=/account/api-keys`. `https://featherless.ai/dashboard` returned HTTP 404.
- `larprouter` keeps its id and is displayed as Lutheos. [Lutheos docs](https://lutheos.com/docs) set the OpenAI base to `https://lutheos.com/v1` and the Anthropic base to `https://lutheos.com`. `GET https://larprouter.com/v1/models` returns HTTP 410 `service_moved`. `lutheos.com` is mapped in addition to `larprouter.com`. The key page is <https://lutheos.com/api-keys> (unsigned GET redirects to `/sign-in`).
- `perplexity` default is Responses at `https://api.perplexity.ai/v1`. The [OpenAI compatibility guide](https://docs.perplexity.ai/docs/agent-api/openai-compatibility) requires that base and `client.responses.create`. `GET /v1/chat/completions` returned HTTP 404, so Chat Completions is not stored. `api.perplexity.ai` stays mapped.
- `liquid-ai` base is `https://api.liquid.ai/openai/v1` per the [quickstart](https://docs.liquid.ai/api/quickstart). `api.liquid.ai` is mapped and `inference.liquid.ai` stays mapped. Docs URL is the quickstart. `https://docs.liquid.ai/` currently redirects to the model library.
- `tencent-token-plan` base is `https://tokenhub.tencentmaas.com/plan/v3`, Anthropic is `https://tokenhub.tencentmaas.com/plan/anthropic`, and Responses is the same OpenAI base (`/plan/v3/responses`). Source: [Token Plan quick start](https://cloud.tencent.com/document/product/1823/130060). `POST` probes of those three routes returned HTTP 405. Inference keeps `api.lkeap.cloud.tencent.com` only for the `/plan` prefix, because Coding Plan still uses that host. `tokenhub.tencentmaas.com` `/plan` maps here; `/v1` remains Tencent TokenHub pay-as-you-go.
- `opencode-zen` base is `https://opencode.ai/inference/openai/v1` (Responses). Chat Completions uses that base, Anthropic uses `https://opencode.ai/inference/anthropic`, and Gemini uses `https://opencode.ai/inference/google/v1beta`. Sources: [Models](https://opencode.ai/docs/console/models/) and [Inference](https://opencode.ai/docs/console/api/inference/). `/docs/zen/` redirects to the models page. `/zen/...` still infers this template.
- `opencode-go` base is `https://opencode.ai/inference/go/openai/v1`. Responses uses that base, Anthropic uses `https://opencode.ai/inference/go/anthropic`, and Gemini uses `https://opencode.ai/inference/go/google/v1beta`. Source: [Go](https://opencode.ai/docs/console/go/). `/docs/go/` redirects there. `/zen/go/...` still infers this template.
- `kilo-gateway` base is `https://ai-gateway.kilo.ai/api/v1` per [AI Gateway](https://kilo.ai/docs/gateway). `ai-gateway.kilo.ai` and `api.kilo.ai` are both mapped. Anonymous `GET /models` on both hosts returned HTTP 200 with the same first model id.
- `longcat` stores Responses at `https://api.longcat.chat/openai/v1`. The [API docs](https://longcat.chat/platform/docs/APIDocs.html) document `POST /openai/v1/responses`.
- `darkbloom` stores Anthropic Messages at `https://api.darkbloom.dev`. The [Messages reference](https://docs.darkbloom.dev/reference/messages) sets that SDK base and calls `POST /v1/messages`.
- `sambanova` stores Responses at `https://api.sambanova.ai/v1` and Anthropic Messages at `https://api.sambanova.ai`. The [OpenAI compatibility](https://docs.sambanova.ai/docs/en/features/openai-compatibility) page documents `POST /v1/responses`. The [Anthropic compatibility](https://docs.sambanova.ai/docs/en/features/anthropic-compatibility.md) page also shows SDK base `https://api.sambanova.ai/v1`, but `POST /v1/messages` is the route that returns the Anthropic error envelope; `POST /v1/v1/messages` does not.
- `cloudflare-workers-ai` exposes Responses on the same account base as Chat Completions. The [OpenAI compatibility](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers-ai/configuration/open-ai-compatibility/) page calls `/v1/responses` under `https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/{account_id}/ai/v1`.
- `stepfun` stores Responses at `https://api.stepfun.com/v1`. [Create response](https://platform.stepfun.com/docs/zh/api-reference/responses/responses-create) documents `POST https://api.stepfun.com/v1/responses`. The concept page redirects to <https://platform.stepfun.com/docs/zh/guides/basic-concepts>.
- `moonshotai` stores Responses at `https://api.moonshot.ai/v1`. Source: <https://platform.kimi.ai/docs/api/responses>. Docs URL is <https://platform.kimi.ai/docs/api/chat> (`platform.moonshot.ai` redirects there). `moonshotai-cn` docs URL is <https://platform.kimi.com/docs/api/chat>. No separate China Responses page was found, so no China Responses endpoint was added.
- `kimi-for-coding` stores Responses at `https://api.kimi.com/coding/v1`. The [Kimi Code docs](https://www.kimi.com/code/docs/en/) also document overseas OpenAI `https://api.kimi.ai/coding/v1` and Anthropic `https://api.kimi.ai/coding/`. `api.kimi.ai` is mapped to this id. The stored China bases are unchanged.

- `xiaomi` stores Responses at `https://api.xiaomimimo.com/v1` and Anthropic Messages at `https://api.xiaomimimo.com/anthropic`. The plan portal is <https://platform.xiaomimimo.com/#/console/plan-manage>. Non-exact URLs on `token-plan-cn`, `token-plan-sgp`, and `token-plan-ams` infer the matching plan template, not pay-as-you-go `xiaomi`.

## Hetzner Experiments Inference — 2026-11-11

`hetzner-experiments` / `Hetzner Experiments Inference` is an official Provider for the [OpenCode with Hetzner Experiments Inference API](https://community.hetzner.com/tutorials/opencode-with-hetzner-inference-api-systemd-sandbox/) tutorial. That page sets the OpenAI-compatible base to `https://inference.hetzner.com/api/v1` and the model id `Qwen/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-FP8`. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on that base, so the request is `POST /api/v1/chat/completions`. The tutorial exports `HETZNER_VLLM_API_KEY` and passes it as the OpenAI-compatible API key. Arca sends `Authorization: Bearer`. No Responses, Anthropic Messages, or Gemini contract is documented, so none is stored.

Probes on 2026-11-11: unsigned `GET https://inference.hetzner.com/api/v1/models`, `GET /v1/models`, `GET /`, `GET /api/v1/not-a-route`, `POST /api/v1/chat/completions`, `POST /api/v1/responses`, `POST /api/v1/messages`, `POST /v1/messages`, and `POST /v1beta/models/Qwen/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-FP8:generateContent` returned HTTP 401 with body `unauthorized`. A bogus Bearer key on `GET /api/v1/models` and `POST /api/v1/chat/completions` returned that same 401. `GET /api/v1/not-a-route` returned the same 401, so a 401 on this host does not prove Responses, Messages, or Gemini. The tutorial documents only the OpenAI-compatible Chat Completions base.

Anonymous `GET /api/v1/models` is not a catalog. No reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered, and this id is not in `EXPECTED_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE`. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is `Qwen/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-FP8`. The [August 17, 2026 announcement](https://www.hetzner.com/blog/inference-experiment/) names launch ids such as `Qwen/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B`, Kimi-K2.7-Code, GLM-5.2, and DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731, and says large models were later paused while smaller Qwen models continued. Those other ids are not seeded. The tutorial gives the seeded id a display name in the OpenCode config. The seed stores no display name and no context length. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

The announcement says the inference API is free of charge for the duration of the experiment and publishes no end date. It describes the product as an experiment that continues on a smaller scale. The catalog does not encode a price or an end date. The template portal is <https://experiments.hetzner.com/> with kind `console`. The tutorial says to log in there, open APPS, select Inference, and click Create API Token. That URL is the experiments console, not a direct key path. An unsigned GET returned 200 (title `Hetzner Experiments`).

Host inference maps `inference.hetzner.com` for every path, including `/api/v1`, `/api/v1/chat/completions`, `/api/v1/models`, and `/v1/models`. `experiments.hetzner.com` is the console. `community.hetzner.com` is the tutorial. `www.hetzner.com` is the announcement. Those stay `custom`, as do `www.inference.hetzner.com`, `api.hetzner.com`, `console.hetzner.com`, and lookalikes.

## Respan — 2026-11-11

`respan` / `Respan` is a gateway Provider for the [Respan gateway quickstart](https://www.respan.ai/docs/ai-gateway/quickstart). [About](https://www.respan.ai/about) says Respan was formerly Keywords AI. The [endpoint list](https://www.respan.ai/docs/ai-gateway/requests/endpoints) documents Chat Completions as `POST https://gateway.respan.ai/v1/chat/completions`. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on `https://gateway.respan.ai/v1`. The quickstart sets `RESPAN_API_KEY` and sends `Authorization: Bearer`. That page says the gateway reads `Authorization: Bearer`, `x-api-key`, or `x-goog-api-key`.

The [Responses](https://www.respan.ai/docs/ai-gateway/requests/responses) page documents `POST https://gateway.respan.ai/responses` and sets the OpenAI SDK base to `https://gateway.respan.ai`, because the client appends `/responses`. A base of `https://gateway.respan.ai/v1` would request `/v1/responses`. The endpoint list also names `POST https://gateway.respan.ai/v1/responses`. Arca stores Responses at `https://gateway.respan.ai`. The [Messages](https://www.respan.ai/docs/ai-gateway/requests/anthropic-messages) page documents `POST https://gateway.respan.ai/v1/messages` and sets the Anthropic SDK base to `https://gateway.respan.ai`, because the client appends `/v1/messages`. A base of `https://gateway.respan.ai/v1` would request `/v1/v1/messages`. Arca stores Anthropic Messages at `https://gateway.respan.ai`. The Messages curl uses `x-api-key` and `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01`, which is the header Arca sends. That page also accepts `Authorization: Bearer`.

Gemini is stored. The [Gemini](https://www.respan.ai/docs/ai-gateway/requests/gemini) page documents `POST https://gateway.respan.ai/v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent` and the curl sends `x-goog-api-key`. That path is Arca's generateContent operation on a base of `https://gateway.respan.ai/v1beta`, and that header is the one Arca sends. The Google GenAI SDK base on that page is `https://gateway.respan.ai`, because the client appends `/v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent`. No Gemini Interactions contract is documented, so Interactions is not stored.

The four stored bases share the origin `https://gateway.respan.ai`, so the stored paths are `/v1/chat/completions`, `/responses`, `/v1/messages`, and `/v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent`.

The [Respan API](https://www.respan.ai/docs/integrations/gateway/respan-api) page documents an alternate OpenAI-compatible base `https://api.respan.ai/api` (`POST /api/chat/completions` with the same Bearer header). That base is not the stored Chat Completions base. Host inference still maps `api.respan.ai`. `api.keywordsai.co` stays `custom`.

Probes on 2026-11-11: unsigned `GET https://gateway.respan.ai/v1/models` returned HTTP 404 `no format served accepts GET /v1/models`. Unsigned `GET /v1beta/models` with `x-goog-api-key` returned HTTP 404 `no format served accepts GET /v1beta/models`. Unsigned `POST /v1/chat/completions`, `POST /chat/completions`, `POST /responses`, and `POST /v1/responses` returned HTTP 403 `Your API key is invalid or expired`. `POST /chat/completions` and `POST /v1/responses` are aliases. Arca stores `/v1/chat/completions` and `/responses`. Unsigned `POST /v1/messages` returned HTTP 403 in Anthropic's error shape. The same route with a bogus `x-api-key` and `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01` returned that same 403, so Arca's Messages header is accepted as a credential. Unsigned `POST /v1beta/models/not-a-model:generateContent` and the same route with a bogus `x-goog-api-key` returned HTTP 403 `PERMISSION_DENIED`, so the Gemini header is accepted as a credential. Unsigned `POST https://api.respan.ai/api/chat/completions` and `POST /api/responses` returned that same invalid-key 403. Unsigned `GET https://api.respan.ai/api/models` returned HTTP 403 `{"detail":"Your API key is invalid or expired, please check your API key in the platform settings."}`.

Anonymous `GET /v1/models` is not a catalog. No reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered, and this id is not in `EXPECTED_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE`. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is `google_gemini_ai/gemini-4-carbon:free` and `meta/muse-ember:free`. The quickstart example `gpt-5.5` is not seeded. The seed stores no display name and no context length. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

The [November 4, 2026 changelog](https://www.respan.ai/docs/changelog/changelog/2026/11/4) says free model promotions use the `:free` suffix and that responses show the remaining allowance. It publishes no end date for these two ids. The public pages for [`google_gemini_ai/gemini-4-carbon:free`](https://www.respan.ai/models/gemini-4-carbon:free) and [`meta/muse-ember:free`](https://www.respan.ai/models/muse-ember:free) show $0.00 per 1M input tokens and $0.00 per 1M output tokens. The catalog does not encode that price or the allowance. The unsuffixed ids `google_gemini_ai/gemini-4-carbon` and `meta/muse-ember` are paid and are not seeded. Those pages state context windows. The seed stores neither a display name nor a context length. The template portal is <https://platform.respan.ai/platform/gateway/api-keys> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned 200 (title `Respan`).

Host inference maps `gateway.respan.ai` and `api.respan.ai` for every path, including `/v1`, `/v1/models`, `/responses`, `/v1/messages`, `/v1beta`, and `/api`. `www.respan.ai` is the documentation site. `platform.respan.ai` is the key app. `respan.ai` stays `custom`, as do `api.keywordsai.co` and lookalikes.

## Maritaca AI — 2026-11-11

`maritaca` / `Maritaca AI` is an official Provider for [Getting started with S5](https://docs.maritaca.ai/en/s5-quick-start). The quickstart sets the OpenAI SDK base to `https://chat.maritaca.ai/api` and documents `POST https://chat.maritaca.ai/api/chat/completions` with `Authorization: Bearer`. That base has no `/v1`. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on `https://chat.maritaca.ai/api`. The [Responses API](https://docs.maritaca.ai/en/responses-api) documents `POST https://chat.maritaca.ai/api/v1/responses` with the same Bearer header. That URL is not the Chat Completions base plus `/responses`. Arca stores Responses at `https://chat.maritaca.ai/api/v1`. The two bases share the origin `https://chat.maritaca.ai`, so the stored paths are `/api/chat/completions` and `/api/v1/responses`. No Anthropic Messages contract is documented. No Gemini contract is documented, so Gemini is not stored.

Probes on 2026-11-10: unsigned `GET /api/models`, `POST /api/chat/completions`, and `POST /api/v1/responses` returned HTTP 403 `{"detail":"Not authenticated"}`. `POST /api/v1/messages` with `x-api-key` and `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01` returned HTTP 404 `{"detail":"Not Found"}`, so Anthropic Messages is not stored.

Anonymous `GET /api/models` is not a catalog. No reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered, and this id is not in `EXPECTED_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE`. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is the current S5 ids, in this order: `S5-free`, `S5`, `S5-br-sp`, and `S5-nano`. The API also accepts the lowercase aliases `s5-free`, `s5`, `s5-br-sp`, and `s5-nano`. Those aliases are not separate rows. Previous-generation ids such as `sabia-4` are not seeded. The [models](https://docs.maritaca.ai/en/models) page states context windows. The seed stores no display name and no context length. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

The models page says `S5-free` is free for a limited time and may be discontinued at any time. It states no end date. The [rate limits](https://docs.maritaca.ai/en/rate-limits) page says `S5-free` is 60 requests per minute, 256k input tokens per minute, and 256k output tokens per minute, in every tier, with no Batch API. The catalog does not encode that allowance. The template portal is <https://plataforma.maritaca.ai/chaves-de-api> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned 200 (title `Plataforma Maritaca AI`).

Host inference maps `chat.maritaca.ai` only when the path is `/api` or under it. The site root is the web chat, not the API. `docs.maritaca.ai` is the documentation site. `plataforma.maritaca.ai` is the key app. `maritaca.ai` and `www.maritaca.ai` stay `custom`, as do lookalikes.

## CrowLLM — 2026-11-11

`crowllm` / `CrowLLM` is a gateway Provider for [CrowLLM](https://crowllm.com/docs). [llms.txt](https://crowllm.com/llms.txt) sets the OpenAI base to `https://crowllm.com/v1` and the Anthropic base to `https://crowllm.com`, because the client appends `/v1/messages`. A base of `https://crowllm.com/v1` would request `/v1/v1/messages`. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on `https://crowllm.com/v1` and stores Anthropic Messages at `https://crowllm.com`. Keys are `sk-...`. Chat Completions uses `Authorization: Bearer`. The [authentication](https://crowllm.com/docs/authentication) page and the [API reference](https://crowllm.com/docs/api-reference) show Messages with `x-api-key` and `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01`, which is the header Arca sends.

Gemini is not stored. [llms-full.txt](https://crowllm.com/llms-full.txt) documents `POST /v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent`, and the API reference lists `POST /v1beta/models/:model:action`. That path is Arca's generateContent operation on a base of `https://crowllm.com/v1beta`. Those same pages say relay calls authenticate with `Authorization: Bearer`. The authentication page limits `x-goog-api-key` to model-listing paths and shows it only as `GET /v1beta/models?key=`. Arca's Gemini client sends `x-goog-api-key` on generateContent, not Bearer, so that stored protocol would not be the documented call.

Responses is not stored. The HTML API reference lists `POST /v1/responses` on the OpenAI base. `llms-full.txt` does not name that route. This template's only OpenAI protocol is Chat Completions.

The authentication page says anonymous `GET /v1/models` is the gateway-wide catalogue. A 2026-11-11 review of that list found about 200 ids, including non-chat rows such as embeddings and images. The generic OpenAI decoder would import those rows, so no reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered and this id is not in `EXPECTED_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE`. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is `qwen-3.8-27b:free`, `deepseek-v4.1-flash:free`, `longcat-2.5:free`, and `gemma-4-31b-it:free`. Other catalogue ids, including `agnes-2.5-flash:free` and `qwen3.8-max`, are not seeded. The seed stores no display name and no context length. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

llms.txt says a shelf of models stays priced at zero, with no card and no trial timer. The public model pages for `qwen-3.8-27b:free`, `longcat-2.5:free`, and `gemma-4-31b-it:free` show $0. `https://crowllm.com` is the Anthropic Messages base, so that exact URL infers to `crowllm`. Paths under `/v1` infer to `crowllm`, including `/v1/models`. The same host is the marketing site. `/docs`, `/console`, `/llms.txt`, `/models`, and `/v1beta` stay `custom`. `www.crowllm.com` stays `custom`, as do lookalikes.

Probes on 2026-11-10: anonymous `GET /v1/models`, `POST /v1/chat/completions`, `POST /v1/responses`, and `POST /v1/messages` returned HTTP 401 `invalid_auth` and asked for `Authorization: Bearer sk-ony-...`. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is sixteen `:tencent` ids, in this order: `tencent/hy4:tencent`, `tencent/hy4-preview:tencent`, `tencent/hy3:tencent`, `deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash:tencent`, `deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash-202605:tencent`, `deepseek/deepseek-v4-pro-202606:tencent`, `deepseek/deepseek-v4-pro-0813:tencent`, `z-ai/glm-5.3:tencent`, `z-ai/glm-5.3-flash:tencent`, `z-ai/glm-5.3-flashx:tencent`, `z-ai/glm-5.2:tencent`, `moonshotai/kimi-k3:tencent`, `moonshotai/kimi-k2.7-code:tencent`, `moonshotai/kimi-k2.7-code-highspeed:tencent`, `minimax/minimax-m3:tencent`, and `minimax/minimax-m3.1-flash:tencent`. The quickstart example `google/gemini-3.8-flash` is not seeded. The seed stores no display name and no context length. These `:tencent` ids are a free promotion through 2026-11-16 23:59 UTC+3, and they close afterwards. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

The catalog does not encode the promotion. An unsigned GET of `https://api.onysoft.com/dashboard/api-keys` returned HTTP 302 to `/login`. The template portal is <https://api.onysoft.com/login> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned 200 (title `Sign In - Onysoft AI Gateway`).

Only `api.onysoft.com` is mapped for host inference. The docs and the sign-in page share that host, so those paths infer to `onysoft`. `onysoft.com` and `www.onysoft.com` are the company site. `www.api.onysoft.com` did not resolve. Lookalikes stay `custom`.

## Coarena — 2026-11-10

`coarena` / `Coarena` is a gateway Provider for the [Coarena Model API](https://coarena.ai/docs). The docs set the OpenAI SDK base to exactly `https://coarena.ai/api/v1` and the Anthropic SDK base to `https://coarena.ai/api` (the client appends `/v1/messages`). Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on `https://coarena.ai/api/v1`, stores Responses on that same base (`client.responses.create` calls `/api/v1/responses`), and stores Anthropic Messages at `https://coarena.ai/api`. Bare `https://coarena.ai/v1` is not the API. Keys are `Authorization: Bearer coa_...` or `x-api-key: coa_...`. No Gemini contract is documented, so Gemini is not stored.

Probes on 2026-11-10: `POST /api/v1/chat/completions`, `POST /api/v1/responses`, and `POST /api/v1/messages` with no key returned HTTP 401 and asked for `Bearer coa_...` or `x-api-key: coa_...`, pointing at `https://coarena.ai/account/api/keys`. A bogus Bearer key on Chat Completions and a bogus `x-api-key` on Messages returned HTTP 401 `invalid_api_key`, so Arca's Messages header is accepted as a credential. `POST https://coarena.ai/v1/chat/completions` and `GET https://coarena.ai/v1/models` returned HTTP 404 HTML. `www.coarena.ai` redirects to the apex. `api.coarena.ai` did not resolve.

Anonymous `GET https://coarena.ai/api/v1/models` returned HTTP 200 `{object: "list", data: [22 entries], has_more: false}`. Every row had `object` and `type` `model`, zero prompt and completion prices, `context_length` 128000, and a `display_name`. Model discovery uses the OpenAI catalog decoder with an optional credential. With no credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is those 22 ids in response order: `claude-fable-5`, `claude-fable-5-1`, `claude-fable-5-2`, `claude-haiku-5-5`, `claude-opus-5-5`, `claude-sonnet-5-5`, `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017`, `gemini-3-8-flash`, `gemini-4-argon`, `gemini-4-carbon`, `glm-5-3-flash`, `gpt-6-1-sol`, `gpt-6-1-terra`, `gpt-6-astra`, `gpt-6-luna`, `grok-4-7`, `hy4`, `kimi-k3`, `kimi-k3-1-pro`, `mimo-v2-6-pro`, `minimax-m3-1-flash`, and `qwen3-8-2-4t-a95b`. Aliases such as `anthropic/claude-fable-5` are not separate rows and are not seeded. The seed stores no display name and no context length. A saved key loads `GET /api/v1/models`. The decoder reads `id`, `display_name`, and `context_length`. This id is in `EXPECTED_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE`.

The docs say free use is $5 of model usage a day, Coarena pays, at 30 requests a minute and 2 calls at once, and no card is required. The catalog does not encode that allowance. The template portal is <https://coarena.ai/account/api/keys> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned 200 (title `API keys · Coarena`).

Host inference maps `coarena.ai` only when the path is `/api` or under it. The site root, `/docs`, `/developers`, `/account/api/keys`, and bare `/v1` stay `custom`. `www.coarena.ai` stays `custom`. Lookalikes stay `custom`.

The template portal is <https://twigpine.com/console/gateway> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned HTTP 307 to `/console/signin?next=%2Fconsole%2Fgateway`.

`vectoree` / `Vectoree` is a gateway Provider for the [Vectoree Model Gateway](https://docs.vectoree.ai/models). The OpenAI client base is exactly `https://vectoree.ai/api/v1`. Bare `https://vectoree.ai/v1` returned HTTP 404 (`Cannot GET /v1`) on 2026-11-10. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on `https://vectoree.ai/api/v1`. The [Responses reference](https://docs.vectoree.ai/api-reference/gateway/openai-responses-api-stateless-subset) documents `POST /api/v1/responses` and says `previous_response_id` is not supported. Arca stores Responses on that same base. The [Messages reference](https://docs.vectoree.ai/api-reference/gateway/anthropic-messages-api) documents `POST /api/v1/messages`. Arca stores Anthropic Messages at `https://vectoree.ai/api`, and the client appends `/v1/messages`. Keys are Bearer `sk-ve-v1-...`. A bogus `x-api-key` on Messages returned `Invalid API key` rather than the missing-key error, so Arca's Messages header is accepted as a credential.

The [Gemini reference](https://docs.vectoree.ai/api-reference/gateway/gemini-generatecontent) documents `POST /api/v1/gemini/generateContent` and says the model is supplied in the JSON body, not the path. A bogus `x-goog-api-key` on `/api/v1/gemini/generateContent` was treated as a missing key, while a bogus Bearer key on that route was `Invalid API key`. That contract is not the stored Gemini protocol, so Gemini is not stored.

Anonymous `GET /api/v1/models` returned HTTP 401 and asked for a scoped `sk-ve-v1-` key. The gateway also serves speech, image, video, and embeddings, so the generic OpenAI decoder is not registered and this id is not in `EXPECTED_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE`. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is only `vectoree/auto`. The docs give that id no separate display name and no context length, so the seed stores neither. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

Sign-up is free. The verified welcome grant is $5. The [billing](https://docs.vectoree.ai/billing) page says there is no monthly subscription and that model calls draw a prepaid organization wallet topped up via Stripe. The catalog does not encode the grant. The template portal is <https://vectoree.ai/dashboard/api-keys> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned 200.

Host inference maps `vectoree.ai` only when the path is `/api` or under it. `https://vectoree.ai/v1`, the site root, and `/dashboard/api-keys` stay `custom`. `docs.vectoree.ai` is the documentation site. `www.vectoree.ai` stays `custom`. `api.vectoree.ai` did not resolve. Lookalikes stay `custom`.

`dahl-inference` / `Dahl Inference` is a gateway Provider for [Dahl Inference](https://inference.dahl.global/docs/quickstart/). No Gemini contract is documented, so Gemini is not stored.

Anonymous `GET /v1/models` on 2026-11-10 returned HTTP 200 with exactly three chat models, and the docs say current models have no vision. No reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is `MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-M2.7`, `zai-org/GLM-5.3-Flash`, and `deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731`. The embedding id `BAAI/bge-m3` is not in this list and is not seeded. The seed stores no display name and no context length. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

The [tokens docs](https://inference.dahl.global/docs/tokens/) say new accounts receive 100 million tokens in the account pool at signup, not on the first key. Tokens must be allocated to a key before use. A new key starts at 0 and returns HTTP 402 until allocated. Free pool tokens burn after 7 consecutive days with no request. Accounts that have topped up keep their tokens. There is no subscription. The 7-day inactivity expiry applies to unused free tokens, not a calendar promo end date. The catalog does not encode that grant. The template portal is <https://inference.dahl.global/account> with kind `console`.

Only `inference.dahl.global` is mapped for host inference. The docs and account pages share that host. `dahl.global` and `www.inference.dahl.global` stay `custom`, as do lookalikes.

No Responses contract is documented for this inference API, so Responses is not stored. No Gemini contract is documented, so Gemini is not stored.

The generic OpenAI decoder would import those rows, so no reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered and the generic `/v1/models` fallback is not used. An older Getting Started FAQ conflicts with this and talks about adding a card for a free tier. The Tier 1 and quota pages are the source used here, not that FAQ. The catalog does not encode the quota. The template portal is <https://console.meganova.ai/api-keys> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned 200. `meganova.ai` without `www` is not the API host.

Only `api.meganova.ai` is mapped for host inference. `docs.meganova.ai` is the documentation site and `console.meganova.ai` is the account app. Those stay `custom`, as do lookalikes.

## Runcrate — 2026-11-10

`runcrate` / `Runcrate` is a gateway Provider for [Runcrate](https://www.runcrate.ai/docs/getting-started/quickstart). Official OpenAI-compatible Chat Completions and Responses use `https://api.runcrate.ai/v1`. Anthropic Messages and Claude Code use `https://api.runcrate.ai`, and the client appends `/v1/messages`. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on `https://api.runcrate.ai/v1`, stores Responses on that same base, and stores Anthropic Messages at `https://api.runcrate.ai`. No Gemini contract is documented, so Gemini is not stored. Keys use the `rc_live_` prefix. `www.runcrate.ai/api/v1` is the infrastructure API, not inference, and is not stored.

Anonymous `GET /v1/models` on 2026-11-10 returned HTTP 200 with 123 entries, including audio, image, and video models. The generic OpenAI decoder would import those rows, so no reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered and the generic `/v1/models` fallback is not used. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The seed is the five official free chat ids, which were present in that list: `deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash`, `zai-org/GLM-5.3-Flash`, `zai-org/GLM-5.3`, `openai/gpt-oss-120b`, and `Qwen/Qwen3.8-Flash`. The seed stores no display name and no context length. An explicit Models list URL on a connection still uses the OpenAI catalog decoder.

The [free inference page](https://www.runcrate.ai/inference/free) says five open chat models are free with a GitHub account at least 30 days old, and no credit card is required. The allowance is shared by the workspace. It has a current-session budget that starts on the first free request and a weekly budget that resets every Monday. Whichever runs out first stops free requests. Past the allowance, workspaces with credit continue. Without credit, requests stop (`free_allowance_used`) or should be retried when capacity is busy (`free_capacity_busy`). This is not a dated promotion. The catalog does not encode that allowance. The template portal is <https://www.runcrate.ai/dashboard/api-keys> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returns 200 and redirects to login.

Only `api.runcrate.ai` is mapped for host inference. `www.runcrate.ai` is the site, the docs host, and the dashboard. `runcrate.ai` stays `custom`, as do lookalikes.

On 2026-11-10, `GET https://moark.com/anthropic` returned HTTP 404 HTML, and an unauthenticated `POST https://moark.com/anthropic/v1/messages` returned HTTP 401 JSON, so the Messages route exists. That origin is not `api.moark.com`. A Provider template stores one origin, so Anthropic Messages is not stored and `moark.com` is not mapped. No Responses or Gemini contract is documented for this template, so neither is stored.

Anonymous `GET https://api.moark.com/v1/models` on 2026-11-10 returned HTTP 200 with 212 entries. The catalog mixes chat with OCR, image, embedding, and other non-chat models. The generic OpenAI decoder would import those rows, so no reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered and the generic `/v1/models` fallback is not used. With or without a credential, Arca does not call the network. The [access-token](https://moark.com/docs/account/access-token) and FAQ docs say the system-created free trial token can be used without buying a resource pack and is not billed. Each user gets 100 free calls per day. Exceeding the cap returns HTTP 400 (`Today the free API access limit exceeded`). The free token is for experience only, not production. Separate paid tokens require a resource pack. No promo end date is published. The catalog does not encode that cap. The template portal is <https://moark.com/settings/access-token> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned 200.

Only `api.moark.com` is mapped for host inference. `moark.com` is the marketing site, the docs host, the token page, and the Anthropic origin. Those paths stay `custom`, as do lookalikes. A bare `GET /v1` on the API host redirects to `/v1/`. The models route itself stayed on `api.moark.com`.

## Bytez — 2026-11-10

`bytez` / `Bytez` is a gateway Provider for the [Bytez OpenAI-compatible API](https://docs.bytez.com/http-reference/examples/openai-compliant/chatCompletionsExample). The Chat Completions and [Responses](https://docs.bytez.com/http-reference/examples/openai-compliant/responsesExample) examples use `https://api.bytez.com/models/v2/openai/v1`. Arca uses Chat Completions as the default on that base and stores Responses on the same base. No Anthropic Messages or Gemini contract is documented for this surface, so neither is stored. Bytez also has many non-chat task APIs. This template is only the OpenAI-compatible chat and Responses surface.

Official curl examples send `Authorization` as the raw key, with no `Bearer` prefix. The OpenAI SDK examples still take `api_key`. This template does not add a second auth header. Callers that follow the curl examples omit `Bearer`.

Anonymous `GET` on that base, on `/models`, and on `/v1/models` returned HTTP 401 on 2026-11-10, so there is no public catalog and no seed. The [billing](https://docs.bytez.com/model-api/docs/billing) page also shows a curl to `https://api.bytez.com/v1/chat/completions`. The documented v2 OpenAI-compatible base is the template default. Host inference still maps the whole `api.bytez.com` host, so that shorter path infers to `bytez` without becoming a second template. No reviewed HTTP discovery spec is registered. A connection with no static seed uses the generic OpenAI `/models` decoder. An anonymous discover call receives the 401. A saved key can load whatever that decoder returns, which is not limited to chat rows.

The billing docs say the Free plan is $0 per month and grants $1 in credits, refreshed every 4 weeks, with credits expiring 4 weeks after grant. The Free plan may run open models up to 7B parameters, with 1 concurrent open-model request, and access to closed-model providers. This is a rolling 4-week credit expiry, not a one-time promo end date. The catalog does not encode the credit or the plan. The template portal is <https://bytez.com/api> with kind `api-key`. An unsigned GET returned 200. `/dashboard` and `/api-keys` on `bytez.com` are HTTP 404.

Only `api.bytez.com` is mapped for host inference. `bytez.com` is the site and the API dashboard. `docs.bytez.com` is the documentation site. Those stay `custom`, as do lookalikes.
