The [models page](https://constellationgate.ai/models) and the live catalog list `constellation/glm-5-3-flash-free` (GLM-5.3-Flash) and `constellation/qwen3-8-flash-free` (Qwen3.8 Flash) at $0 input and output with a 1M context window; [Plans](https://docs.constellationgate.ai/billing/plans/) describes a Free plan with no credit card and a Pro plan at $20 per user per month, and says pay-through-Gate requests need a funded balance. No models are hard-coded because the catalog is live and the free lineup is not documented as stable. Only `gateway.constellationgate.ai` is mapped for host inference; `constellationgate.ai`, `app.constellationgate.ai`, `api.constellationgate.ai` (the keys-management API), and `docs.constellationgate.ai` resolve to `custom`.

## AkashML — 2026-10-19

`akashml` is a gateway Provider for [AkashML](https://akashml.com/), managed inference for open models (GLM 5.3, Kimi K3, Qwen3.8 27B, Qwen3.6 35B A3B, GPT OSS 120B/20B, Llama 3.3 70B) running on the Akash Network, billed from pay-as-you-go credits with trial credits on signup. The [Introduction](https://akashml.com/docs/getting-started/introduction) states that the API is OpenAI-compatible at `api.akashml.com`, calls `POST https://api.akashml.com/v1/chat/completions` with `Authorization: Bearer YOUR_API_KEY`, configures the OpenAI SDK with `baseURL: "https://api.akashml.com/v1"`, and has users create keys under [Settings → API Keys](https://akashml.com/docs/platform/settings) in the [playground](https://playground.akashml.com/settings), so Arca uses that Base URL with Chat Completions as the default. The [Anthropic SDK reference](https://akashml.com/docs/api-reference/anthropic) and [Claude Code guide](https://akashml.com/docs/guides/claude-code) document an Anthropic Messages-compatible `POST https://api.akashml.com/anthropic/v1/messages` with the same Bearer key (keys prefixed `akml-`) and set `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` to `https://api.akashml.com/anthropic`, so Arca stores Anthropic Messages at that URL. On that endpoint model IDs alias `/` as `--` (for example `zai-org--GLM-5.3`); the OpenAI endpoints keep slashed IDs. No Responses or Gemini format is documented. Probes without a key: `POST /v1/chat/completions` and `GET /v1/models` return 401 `Missing authorization header` (a bogus key gets `Invalid or expired API key`), `POST /anthropic/v1/messages` returns 401 `authentication_error`, `GET /anthropic` returns 200 `{"status":"ok"}`, and `/v1/responses` returns 404.

Model discovery uses the OpenAI catalog decoder with a required credential: the [List models reference](https://akashml.com/docs/api-reference/models/get-v1-models) documents `GET /v1/models` with Bearer auth, returning an OpenAI-shaped `data` array (`id`, `name`, `context_length`, `max_output_length`, `input_modalities`, `pricing`), and the unauthenticated probe returns 401. No models are hard-coded because the catalog is live. Only `api.akashml.com` is mapped for host inference; `akashml.com`, `www.akashml.com`, and `playground.akashml.com` resolve to `custom`.

## Fikra API — 2026-10-18

`fikra` is a gateway Provider for [Fikra API](https://fikraapi.co.ke/), an OpenAI-compatible model gateway operated by Lacesse Ventures that is paid from a prepaid wallet via M-Pesa, other mobile money, or card. The [documentation](https://docs.fikraapi.co.ke/) lists the Base URL `https://api.fikraapi.co.ke/v1` with `Authorization: Bearer` keys prefixed `fk_live_`, generated from the [dashboard](https://fikraapi.co.ke/dashboard); the [Endpoints](https://docs.fikraapi.co.ke/endpoints) page documents only `POST https://api.fikraapi.co.ke/v1/chat/completions`, so Arca uses that Base URL with Chat Completions as the default and no additional endpoints. The [free model page](https://fikraapi.co.ke/models/fikra-free-deepseek) shows the same OpenAI SDK setup for `fikra-free-deepseek` ($0 input and output, running `deepseek-v4-flash-0731` with 1M context). Probes without a key: `POST /v1/chat/completions` returns 403 `Not authenticated` (a malformed key gets `Invalid API key format.`), while `POST /v1/responses` and `POST /v1/messages` return 405, so no Responses or Anthropic Messages endpoint is exposed and no Gemini format is documented.

Model discovery uses the OpenAI catalog decoder with an optional credential: the [Authentication](https://docs.fikraapi.co.ke/authentication) page calls `GET https://api.fikraapi.co.ke/v1/models` with the Bearer key, and the same request without a key returns HTTP 200 with an OpenAI-shaped `data` array (`id`, `name`, `pricing`, `context_window`, `max_output_tokens`), including `fikra-free-deepseek`, `fikra-pro-20b`, `fikra-auto`, and third-party models. No models are hard-coded because the catalog is live. Only `api.fikraapi.co.ke` is mapped for host inference; `fikraapi.co.ke`, `www.fikraapi.co.ke`, and `docs.fikraapi.co.ke` resolve to `custom`.

## SenseNova Token Plan — 2026-10-18

`sensenova` is an official Provider for SenseTime's SenseNova (商汤日日新) LLM API, sold as the [Token Plan](https://www.sensetime.com/en/news/sensenova-u1-5-lite-token-plan-20260911-1741). The [API docs](https://platform.sensenova.cn/docs) state that "all API requests are based on `https://token.sensenova.cn/v1`", call `POST /v1/chat/completions` with `Authorization: Bearer $SENSENOVA_API_KEY`, and have users create a key starting with `sk-` under [Console → API Keys](https://platform.sensenova.cn/console/keys), so Arca uses that Base URL with Chat Completions as the default. The same docs document an OpenAI Responses-compatible `POST https://token.sensenova.cn/v1/responses` (stateless only: no stored responses, `previous_response_id`, or non-function built-in tools) and an Anthropic Messages-compatible `POST https://token.sensenova.cn/v1/messages` that shares the same key and Bearer header; the Claude Code guide sets `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` to `https://token.sensenova.cn` without `/v1`. Arca therefore stores Responses at `https://token.sensenova.cn/v1` and Anthropic Messages at `https://token.sensenova.cn`. Unauthenticated probes of `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/responses`, `/v1/messages`, and `GET /v1/models` return HTTP 401 (`Authorization Not Found`, or `Forbidden` for a bogus key), while an unknown `/v1/` path returns 404.

Model discovery uses the OpenAI catalog decoder with a required credential: the docs' List Models section documents `GET https://token.sensenova.cn/v1/models` with the Bearer key, returning a `data` array whose entries carry `id`, `input_modalities`, `context_length`, and `max_output_length` (for example `sensenova-6.8-flash-lite`: text and image input, 262144 context, 65536 max output). The documented models are `sensenova-6.8-flash-lite` (chat, also on Responses and Messages), the image models `sensenova-u1.5-lite` and `sensenova-u1.5-fast` on `POST /v1/images/generations` and `/v1/images/edits`, and hosted third-party models (`deepseek-v4-flash`, `deepseek-flash`, `glm-5.2`, `kimi-k3`); none are hard-coded because the catalog is live. The console's Token Plan page describes a free public beta with a per-model quota of 1,500 calls every 5 hours, counted per model and shared across all keys on the account; this quota is not encoded. Only `token.sensenova.cn` is mapped for host inference; `sensenova.cn`, `www.sensenova.cn`, and `platform.sensenova.cn` resolve to `custom`.

## Bilibili Index-Translate — 2026-10-18

`bilibili-index-translate` is an official Provider for the free public API that Bilibili's Index team runs for its translation-specialized Index-Translate models (150 languages, image translation; [repository](https://github.com/bilibili/Index-Translate), [weights](https://huggingface.co/collections/IndexTeam/index-translate)). The README's [Free Online API](https://github.com/bilibili/Index-Translate#option-1-free-online-api-zero-gpu-setup) section calls `POST https://index-translate.bilibili.com/v1/chat/completions` (text and OpenAI `image_url` input) and `POST https://index-translate.bilibili.com/v1/responses` without any `Authorization` header, so Arca uses the Base URL `https://index-translate.bilibili.com/v1` with Chat Completions as the default plus Responses on the same Base URL. Probes without a key returned HTTP 200 from both paths, a bogus `Authorization: Bearer` header is ignored, and `POST /v1/messages` returns 404, so no Anthropic Messages endpoint is exposed.

There is no key or signup, so the template uses the `none` auth requirement like the local `ollama`, `lm-studio`, and `vllm` templates, and its portal is the [landing page](https://index-translate.bilibili.com/) with the `setup` kind. Model discovery uses the OpenAI catalog decoder with the optional credential policy: anonymous `GET /v1/models` returns the OpenAI list shape with one entry, `Index-Translate-35B-A3B` (text and image input, text output, `context_length` 24576, `max_output_tokens` 4096, `function_calling: false`). The README's client script also passes `-m Index-Translate-2B` / `Index-Translate-9B`, and the server's unknown-model error lists further public ids (`index-mt-2b`, `index-mt-9b`, and `-syllable` variants), but none of these appear in `/v1/models`, so no model is hard-coded. The model has no tool calling, so it suits translation workflows rather than agentic coding. Only `index-translate.bilibili.com` is mapped for host inference; `bilibili.com` and `www.bilibili.com` resolve to `custom`. The README publishes no rate limit or service-level terms.

## OrcaRouter — 2026-10-17

`orcarouter` is a gateway Provider for [OrcaRouter](https://www.orcarouter.ai), a multi-provider router whose [llms.txt](https://www.orcarouter.ai/llms.txt) describes it as aggregating OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and other vendors "behind one OpenAI-compatible API", with provider-prefixed model ids such as `deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash` and the `$0` variant `deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash-free`. The [Quickstart](https://docs.orcarouter.ai/getting-started/quickstart.md) and [OpenAI SDK guide](https://docs.orcarouter.ai/compatibility/openai-sdk.md) give the Base URL `https://api.orcarouter.ai/v1` and call `POST /v1/chat/completions` with `Authorization: Bearer sk-orca-...`, so Arca uses that Base URL with Chat Completions as the default. [OpenAI-compatible HTTP](https://docs.orcarouter.ai/native-formats/openai-compat.md) documents `POST /v1/responses` on the same Base URL for every chat model. The [Anthropic SDK guide](https://docs.orcarouter.ai/compatibility/anthropic-sdk.md) sets `base_url="https://api.orcarouter.ai"` (the SDK appends `/v1/messages`, and `x-api-key` is accepted), and the [Google GenAI SDK guide](https://docs.orcarouter.ai/compatibility/google-genai-sdk.md) with [Gemini native HTTP](https://docs.orcarouter.ai/native-formats/gemini.md) documents `POST https://api.orcarouter.ai/v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent`; like `unorouter`, Arca stores Anthropic Messages at `https://api.orcarouter.ai` and Gemini at `https://api.orcarouter.ai/v1beta`. Unauthenticated `POST` to `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/responses`, and `/v1/messages` returns HTTP 401 (`Invalid API key`, type `orcarouter_api_error`).

Model discovery uses the OpenAI catalog decoder with the optional credential policy: [Models](https://docs.orcarouter.ai/getting-started/models.md) documents `GET https://api.orcarouter.ai/v1/models` in the OpenAI list shape (`{object: "list", data: [{id, object, owned_by, supported_endpoint_types}]}`), and [Free Models](https://docs.orcarouter.ai/routing/free-models.md) says the list accepts anonymous callers while a request carrying an invalid key gets a 401; an anonymous probe returned the catalog. Keys (`sk-orca-…`) are issued from the [Dashboard](https://www.orcarouter.ai/console) ([Get an API key](https://docs.orcarouter.ai/getting-started/get-api-key.md)), which is the template portal; the model pages' samples export them as `ORCAROUTER_API_KEY`. Only `api.orcarouter.ai` is mapped for host inference; `www.orcarouter.ai` and `docs.orcarouter.ai` resolve to `custom`. The [offers page](https://www.orcarouter.ai/offers) lists rotating voucher, student, and hackathon credits plus a rotating `-free` model lineup with tiered rate limits, so no free credit or free model is encoded.

## Meta Model API — 2026-10-17

`meta` / `Meta Model API` is an official Provider for Meta's first-party inference API ([docs overview](https://dev.meta.ai/docs/overview)); the id and display name match the provider key and name in Meta's own OpenCode config. The [Quickstart](https://dev.meta.ai/docs/quickstart) gives the Base URL `https://api.meta.ai/v1` for OpenAI-compatible tools, calls `POST https://api.meta.ai/v1/responses` with `Authorization: Bearer $MODEL_API_KEY`, and points Anthropic-format harnesses at `https://api.meta.ai`. [Choosing an API](https://dev.meta.ai/docs/protocols.md) says Responses, Chat Completions, and Messages run the same models with the same bearer auth and price, calls Responses "the recommended default for new work", notes that Chat Completions does not carry reasoning across turns for external keys, and says the Anthropic SDK should use the base host `https://api.meta.ai` (it appends `/v1/messages`). Arca therefore defaults to Responses at `https://api.meta.ai/v1`, with Chat Completions on the same Base URL and Anthropic Messages at `https://api.meta.ai`. Unauthenticated `POST` to `/v1/responses`, `/v1/chat/completions`, and `/v1/messages` and `GET /v1/models` return HTTP 401 (`invalid_api_key` / `authentication_error`).

Keys come from the Model API dashboard at <https://dev.meta.ai/> → API keys ([Authentication](https://dev.meta.ai/docs/authentication.md)); Meta's examples export them as `MODEL_API_KEY`, which no SDK reads automatically. The [pricing page](https://dev.meta.ai/docs/pricing-rate-limits) lists `muse-spark-1.3`, `muse-spark-1.2`, and `muse-spark-1.1` in Standard mode, the discounted Contributor-mode variants `muse-spark-1.3-contributor` and `muse-spark-1.2-contributor` (content may be used to train Meta models), and `muse-wonder-1.0`. Model discovery uses the OpenAI adapter with a required credential: [List models](https://dev.meta.ai/docs/api-reference/models/list-models.md) documents `GET https://api.meta.ai/v1/models`, and its [schema](https://dev.meta.ai/docs/api-reference/models/schemas.md) returns `{object: "list", data: [{id, object: "model", created, owned_by}]}`. Only `api.meta.ai` is mapped for host inference; `meta.ai`, `www.meta.ai`, and `dev.meta.ai` resolve to `custom`. The docs pages checked here do not state a regional restriction or a signup credit, so neither is encoded.

## Agnes AI — 2026-10-17

`agnes-ai` is an official Provider for Agnes AI's hosted API for its own text models ([product site](https://agnes-ai.com)). The [Agnes 3.0 Flash guide](https://wiki.agnes-ai.com/en/docs/agnes-30-flash) gives the Base URL `https://apihub.agnes-ai.com/v1` and documents `POST /v1/chat/completions` and `POST /v1/responses` with `Authorization: Bearer <API_KEY>`, plus the Anthropic-compatible `POST https://apihub.agnes-ai.com/v1/messages` with `x-api-key` and `anthropic-version`. The [Agnes 2.5 Flash guide](https://wiki.agnes-ai.com/en/docs/agnes-25-flash) lists the same Base URL and the same three endpoints, and the [Quickstart](https://wiki.agnes-ai.com/en/docs/quickstart) uses `POST https://apihub.agnes-ai.com/v1/chat/completions`. Arca uses that Base URL with Chat Completions as the default, plus Responses on the same Base URL and Anthropic Messages at `https://apihub.agnes-ai.com` (the Anthropic client appends `/v1/messages`). Unauthenticated `POST` to all three paths and `GET /v1/models` return HTTP 401 (`Token not provided`, type `AgnesAI_error`).

The guides document `agnes-3.0-flash` (512K context, 65,536 max output tokens, text and image-URL input) and `agnes-2.5-flash`. The [pricing page](https://wiki.agnes-ai.com/en/docs/pricing) lists both as currently $0 for cached input, input, and output (list prices $0.005 / $0.05 / $0.15 per 1M tokens), bills `agnes-2.5-pro` and `agnes-2.5-pro-beta` at published prices, and marks `agnes-3.0-pro` as coming soon; the promotion has no published end date, so no free tier is encoded. No model discovery adapter is registered: the docs only describe third-party tools' "Fetch Model List" buttons and never document `GET /v1/models` or its response shape. Only `apihub.agnes-ai.com` is mapped for host inference; its root redirects to the console at <https://platform.agnes-ai.com/>, which is the template portal (the [Claude CLI guide](https://wiki.agnes-ai.com/en/docs/cid3) links it for API keys). `agnes-ai.com`, `platform.agnes-ai.com`, and `wiki.agnes-ai.com` resolve to `custom`.

`agnes-ai-cn` is the mainland-China deployment, kept separate like `moonshotai-cn` because it has its own host, console, and CNY pricing. The Simplified Chinese [Agnes 3.0 Flash guide](https://wiki.agnes-ai.cn/zh-Hans/docs/agnes-30-flash) gives the Base URL `https://api.agnes-ai.cn/v1` with the same three endpoints, and the [Chinese Quickstart](https://wiki.agnes-ai.cn/zh-Hans/docs/quickstart) uses `POST https://api.agnes-ai.cn/v1/chat/completions`; the [Chinese pricing page](https://wiki.agnes-ai.cn/zh-Hans/docs/pricing) lists the two Flash models at ¥0. Unauthenticated `POST` to all three paths and `GET /v1/models` on `api.agnes-ai.cn` return HTTP 401 (`未提供令牌`). Its root redirects to <https://platform.agnes-ai.cn/>, the template portal. Note that the Chinese-language pages on `wiki.agnes-ai.com/zh-hans` still show the international host; only `wiki.agnes-ai.cn` documents `api.agnes-ai.cn`.

## Coral Bricks — 2026-10-17

`coralbricks` is a gateway Provider for [Coral Bricks](https://www.coralbricks.ai/), an inference platform that serves open models (for example `glm-5.3-fast` and `deepseek-v4.1-flash-fast`) on its own GPUs behind an OpenAI-compatible API. The site's [llms.txt](https://www.coralbricks.ai/llms.txt) states the Base URL `https://inference.coralbricks.ai/v1`, and the [API reference](https://www.coralbricks.ai/docs) (raw markdown at [`/docs.md`](https://www.coralbricks.ai/docs.md)) says Chat Completions, Responses, and Responses background mode all share that Base URL with `Authorization: Bearer <CORAL_API_KEY>` (`cb_…` keys). Arca uses that Base URL with Chat Completions as the default, plus Responses on the same Base URL. The [Claude Code guide](https://www.coralbricks.ai/docs/claude-code) documents the Anthropic Messages API at `POST /v1/messages` with `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=https://inference.coralbricks.ai` (no `/v1`), accepting `x-api-key` or `Authorization: Bearer`, so Arca stores Anthropic Messages at `https://inference.coralbricks.ai`. Unauthenticated `POST` to `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/responses`, and `/v1/messages` returns HTTP 401.

Model discovery uses `GET /v1/models` (OpenAI list shape) with the OpenAI catalog decoder and the required credential policy. On 2026-11-10 an anonymous `GET /v1/models` returned HTTP 200. The docs still treat capability fields as authoritative for each key, so the credential policy was not changed. Only `inference.coralbricks.ai` is mapped for host inference; `www.coralbricks.ai` is the marketing site and console. Accounts sign up at <https://www.coralbricks.ai/register> and create keys at <https://www.coralbricks.ai/api-keys>, which is the template portal. The docs describe pay-per-token billing from a prepaid credit balance; the $20 new-account trial credit is only announced on the official [X account](https://x.com/coralbricksdev/status/2111310266168172587), so no free credit is encoded.

## RunAnywhere — 2026-10-16

`runanywhere` is a gateway Provider for Wally Cloud, RunAnywhere's hosted inference service for open models (for example `glm-5.3-flash`, `deepseek-v4.1-flash`, and `mimo-v2.6-pro`) running on RunAnywhere's hardware ([product site](https://runanywhere.ai/)). The [Wally Cloud overview](https://docs.runanywhere.ai/wally-cloud) calls the endpoint OpenAI-compatible, and the [API reference](https://docs.runanywhere.ai/wally-cloud/api) gives the Base URL `https://inference.runanywhere.ai/v1` with `Authorization: Bearer sk-runa-…` and documents `POST /v1/chat/completions`, so Arca uses that Base URL with the Chat Completions protocol. The gateway's own [OpenAPI spec](https://inference.runanywhere.ai/openapi.json) lists only `/v1/chat/completions` and `/v1/models` as inference routes; on 2026-11-10 an anonymous `GET /v1/models` returned HTTP 200 and included `glm-5.3-flash`. Discovery still uses the required-credential policy because the list-models reference describes the key's catalog. `/v1/responses` and `/v1/messages` are not documented, so no Responses or Anthropic Messages endpoint is exposed.

Model discovery uses the documented, key-scoped `GET /v1/models` (OpenAI list shape per the [list models reference](https://docs.runanywhere.ai/api-reference/list-models-available-to-the-current-key)) with the OpenAI catalog decoder and the required credential policy; the docs treat that response as the authority because entitlement is per key and per environment. Cloud keys are separate from on-device `runa_prod_` SDK keys. Only `inference.runanywhere.ai` is mapped for host inference; `runanywhere.ai` is the marketing site and `console.runanywhere.ai` is the account console (Google or GitHub sign-in), where Cloud keys are created and which is the template portal. Usage bills against prepaid credits; a signup credit was not confirmed in the docs or site, so none is encoded.

## UnoRouter — 2026-10-16

`unorouter` is a gateway Provider for [UnoRouter](https://unorouter.com/en), an OpenAI-compatible multi-model gateway whose [model catalog](https://unorouter.com/en/models) routes to many upstream vendors and marks no-cost models with a `:free` suffix (see [Models & Pricing](https://unorouter.com/en/docs/platform/models-and-pricing)). The [Quickstart](https://unorouter.com/en/docs/platform/quickstart) gives the Base URL `https://api.unorouter.com/v1` with `Authorization: Bearer <API_KEY>` and documents `POST /v1/chat/completions`, so Arca uses that Base URL with Chat Completions as the default. The [Codex guide](https://unorouter.com/en/docs/integrations/codex) lists Responses on the same Base URL (`wire_api = "responses"`). The [Claude Code guide](https://unorouter.com/en/docs/integrations/claude-code) sets `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=https://api.unorouter.com`, and the [Gemini CLI guide](https://unorouter.com/en/docs/integrations/gemini-cli) sets `GOOGLE_GEMINI_BASE_URL=https://api.unorouter.com`, with the Google GenAI SDK appending its own `/v1beta/models/...` path; like `aihubmix`, Arca stores that Gemini endpoint as `https://api.unorouter.com/v1beta`.

Model discovery uses the documented, key-required `GET /v1/models` ([Platform API](https://unorouter.com/en/docs/platform/platform-api)) with the OpenAI catalog decoder and the required credential policy; the public `/api/pricing/catalog` is not used. On 2026-11-10 an anonymous `GET https://api.unorouter.com/v1/models` returned HTTP 200 with an OpenAI list. Discovery still uses the required credential policy; that policy was not changed in this pass. Only `api.unorouter.com` is mapped for host inference; `unorouter.com` hosts the web app, docs, and catalog. API keys are created on the Tokens page at <https://unorouter.com/en/token>, which is the template portal.

## Atria — 2026-10-15

`atria` is an official Provider for Atria ASI's hosted API for its Atria Dawn model (Shanghai AI Laboratory; [product site](https://atria-asi.ai/)). The [API homepage](https://api.atria-asi.ai/) announces the official `Atria-Dawn` model ID (the earlier `Atria-Dawn-Preview` ID stays compatible), a 512K-token context window, and support for Chat Completions, Messages, and Responses. The [docs](https://api.atria-asi.ai/docs) document `POST https://api.atria-asi.ai/v1/chat/completions` and `POST https://api.atria-asi.ai/v1/responses` with `Authorization: Bearer <ATRIA_API_KEY>`, and an Anthropic-compatible `POST https://api.atria-asi.ai/v1/messages` with `x-api-key`. The Claude Code guide sets `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=https://api.atria-asi.ai` (no `/v1`). Like `trustedrouter`, Arca uses `https://api.atria-asi.ai/v1` with Responses as the default, plus Chat Completions on the same Base URL and Anthropic Messages at `https://api.atria-asi.ai`. Model IDs are case-sensitive (`Atria-Dawn`); the input is text-only.

Unauthenticated `POST` to all three inference paths and `GET /v1/models` return HTTP 401 (`invalid_api_key`), and an unknown path returns 404. No model discovery adapter is registered because the docs do not document `/v1/models`. Only `api.atria-asi.ai` is mapped for host inference; `atria-asi.ai` is the product site. Accounts sign in with Google, and API keys are created at <https://api.atria-asi.ai/console/keys>, which is the template portal. The homepage notes that website API quotas were recently tightened and recommends the [Duanyan (端砚) Token Plan](https://discovery.intern-ai.org.cn/token-plan) for mainland-China networks or heavier use; that plan is a separate service and is not part of this template. Rate limits are per account across all keys and all three endpoints, with 429 plus `Retry-After` / `x-rpm-limit` / `x-rpm-remaining` headers.

## Experiential Labs — 2026-10-14

`experiential-labs` is a gateway Provider for Experiential Labs, an OpenAI-compatible model gateway that currently offers a free allowance for Gemini 4 Argon to eligible accounts (see the [model page](https://platform.experientiallabs.ai/models/gemini-4-argon)). The [docs overview](https://platform.experientiallabs.ai/docs) gives the Base URL `https://api.experientiallabs.ai/v1` and documents `POST /v1/chat/completions`, so Arca uses that Base URL with the Chat Completions protocol. The [OpenAI compatibility guide](https://platform.experientiallabs.ai/docs/openai-compatibility) also documents `POST /v1/responses`, and the [Anthropic API guide](https://platform.experientiallabs.ai/docs/anthropic) documents `POST /v1/messages`; like `beatapi`, the template exposes only Chat Completions.

Requests use `Authorization: Bearer <EXPLABS_API_KEY>` with an `xpl_` key ([authentication](https://platform.experientiallabs.ai/docs/authentication)); requests without a key return HTTP 401 (`invalid_key`). Model discovery uses the documented, key-required `GET /v1/models` (see [the core loop](https://platform.experientiallabs.ai/docs/core-loop)) with the OpenAI catalog decoder and the required credential policy. Only `api.experientiallabs.ai` is mapped for host inference; `platform.experientiallabs.ai` hosts the web app, docs, and [model catalog](https://platform.experientiallabs.ai/models). API keys are managed at <https://platform.experientiallabs.ai/api-keys>, which is the template portal.

## Liquid AI — 2026-10-13

The live base URL moved to `https://api.liquid.ai/openai/v1` on 2026-11-10. See Template corrections above.

`liquid-ai` is an official Provider for Liquid AI's hosted inference of its LFM models. The [LFM2.5-24B-A2B announcement](https://www.liquid.ai/blog/lfm2-5-24b-a2b) directs API users to [Liquid Console](https://console.liquid.ai/), which grants $2 of signup credit and lists the model at $0.10/1M input tokens and $1.00/1M output tokens. The [docs](https://docs.liquid.ai/) cover models and self-hosting but do not document a hosted API reference. The inference host's own [OpenAPI spec](https://inference.liquid.ai/openapi.json) lists `POST /v1/chat/completions`, `POST /v1/completions`, and `GET /v1/models` with HTTP Bearer authentication, so Arca uses the Base URL `https://inference.liquid.ai/v1` with the Chat Completions protocol. No Responses or Anthropic Messages endpoint exists (`/v1/responses` returns 404), so none is exposed.

Unauthenticated `POST /v1/chat/completions` and `GET /v1/models` return HTTP 403 (`Not authenticated`), and an unknown path returns 404. No model discovery adapter is registered: the spec gives `/v1/models` an empty response schema, so the list shape could not be verified without a key. Only `inference.liquid.ai` is mapped for host inference. Accounts and API keys are managed at <https://console.liquid.ai/>, which is the template portal.

## BeatAPI — 2026-10-13

`beatapi` is a gateway Provider for BeatAPI, a unified OpenAI-compatible API platform that currently offers limited-time free models. The [quick guide](https://docs.beatapi.io/quick-guide) gives the API host `https://api.beatapi.io`, and the [text API reference](https://docs.beatapi.io/text-api/claude) documents `POST https://api.beatapi.io/v1/chat/completions`, so Arca uses the Base URL `https://api.beatapi.io/v1` with the Chat Completions protocol. The same reference also lists `/v1/responses` and `/v1/messages`; like `codecraft`, the template exposes only Chat Completions.

Requests use `Authorization: Bearer <BEATAPI_API_KEY>`; requests without a key return HTTP 401 (`missing_api_key`). Model discovery uses the documented `GET /v1/models` (OpenAI list shape, see the [text API reference](https://docs.beatapi.io/text-api/claude)) with the OpenAI catalog decoder and the required credential policy. Only `api.beatapi.io` is mapped for host inference; the apex `beatapi.io` is the marketing site. Accounts and API keys are managed at <https://beatapi.io/dashboard/get-started>, which is the template portal.

## CodeCraft API — 2026-10-13

`codecraft` is a gateway Provider for CodeCraft API, an OpenAI-compatible gateway with a permanent free tier (1M tokens/month, no credit card; see [pricing](https://codecraftapi.com/pricing)). The [docs](https://codecraftapi.com/docs) give the base URL `https://codecraftapi.com/v1` and document `POST /chat/completions`, so Arca uses that Base URL with the Chat Completions protocol. No Responses or Anthropic Messages endpoint is documented, so none is exposed.

The [authentication guide](https://codecraftapi.com/docs/authentication) requires `Authorization: Bearer cc_...`; requests without a valid key return HTTP 401 in the OpenAI error envelope. Model discovery uses the documented `GET /v1/models` ([models reference](https://codecraftapi.com/docs/models)) with the OpenAI catalog decoder and the required credential policy; the key needs the default `models:read` scope. The public catalog is at <https://codecraftapi.com/models>. Accounts are created at <https://codecraftapi.com/register>, which is the template portal; keys are managed in the dashboard after sign-in.

## Sarashina — 2026-10-13

`sarashina` is an official Provider for SB Intuitions' (a SoftBank subsidiary) Sarashina API, which opened as a free beta on 2026-10-13 ([press release](https://prtimes.jp/main/html/rd/p/000000002.000141057.html)). The [Chat Completions reference](https://docs.sarashina.ai/api-reference/chat-completions) documents `POST https://api.sarashina.ai/v1/chat/completions` with `Authorization: Bearer ${SARASHINA_API_KEY}`, giving the Arca Base URL `https://api.sarashina.ai/v1` with the Chat Completions protocol. No Responses or Anthropic Messages endpoint is documented, so none is exposed. Accounts (Google sign-in) and API keys are managed at <https://sarashina.ai/>, which is the template portal; the [introduction](https://docs.sarashina.ai/introduction) is the docs link.

The [model list](https://docs.sarashina.ai/models) documents the chat models `sarashina3-mini` and `sarashina3-nano` (128k context) and the moderation model `sarashina3-guard`. Embeddings, rerank, and translation endpoints are out of scope. [Rate limits](https://docs.sarashina.ai/rate-limits) apply per account.

Compatibility caveats from the reference: OpenAI fields the API does not support, such as `logprobs` and `stream_options`, are rejected with HTTP 400 (`Invalid JSON or disallowed fields`); `max_completion_tokens` is capped at 8000; and the object form of `tool_choice` is unsupported. Arca only writes the Agent configuration and has no per-provider request-field filter, so Agents that send these fields by default may fail against this endpoint.

No model discovery adapter is registered: the docs do not document `GET /v1/models`, and an unauthenticated request returned HTTP 403.

## Free-offer Provider expansion — 2026-10-13

Adds 12 templates found in a curated list of free model/API offers (118 templates including Custom, after Sarashina). Every Base URL below is copied from the vendor's own documentation; each documented path was also probed without credentials and returned an authentication or request-validation error rather than 404. Only protocols that are documented are exposed. Model IDs are left to each account's catalog or manual entry.

| Arca Provider ID | Base URL / protocols | Model discovery | Official sources |
|---|---|---|---|
| `clean-apis` | `https://cleanapis.com/v1` (Chat Completions only) | `GET /v1/models`, credential required | [Getting started](https://cleanapis.com/docs/getting-started), [Coding agents](https://cleanapis.com/docs/coding-agents) |
| `dolphin` | `https://lighthouse.dphn.ai/run/text-v/v1` (Chat Completions only) | `GET /models`, credential required | [Coupon credits quick-start](https://dphn.ai/docs/coupon-credits) |
| `iteracompute` | `https://api.iteracompute.com/v1` (Chat Completions default, Responses) | public `GET /v1/models` | [API documentation](https://iteracompute.com/docs.html) |
| `llmtr` | `https://llmtr.com/v1` (Chat Completions only) | public `GET /v1/models` | [Docs](https://llmtr.com/docs/), [Chat Completions](https://llmtr.com/docs/gateway/chat-completions/), [llms.txt](https://llmtr.com/llms.txt) |
| `munito` | `https://api.munito.ai/inference/v1` (Responses default, Chat Completions); Anthropic `https://api.munito.ai/inference` | public `GET /models` | [Docs](https://docs.munito.ai/), [API reference](https://docs.munito.ai/api), [Claude Code](https://docs.munito.ai/claude-code), [Regions](https://docs.munito.ai/regions) |
| `nous-portal` | `https://inference-api.nousresearch.com/v1` (Chat Completions only) | public `GET /v1/models` | [API docs](https://portal.nousresearch.com/api-docs), [OpenAPI](https://portal.nousresearch.com/api/openapi), [Hermes Agent integration](https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/docs/integrations/nous-portal) |
| `ppq` | `https://api.ppq.ai/v1` (Responses default, Chat Completions); Anthropic `https://api.ppq.ai` | public `GET /v1/models` | [API docs](https://ppq.ai/api-docs), [llms.txt](https://ppq.ai/llms.txt) |
| `river` | `https://api.river.ai/v1` (Responses default, Chat Completions) | `GET /v1/models`, credential required | [Inference quickstart](https://docs.river.ai/guides/inference/), [API controls](https://docs.river.ai/guides/inference-controls/) |
| `trustedrouter` | `https://api.trustedrouter.com/v1` (Responses default, Chat Completions); Anthropic `https://api.trustedrouter.com` | public `GET /v1/models` | [API docs](https://trustedrouter.com/docs), [Agent setup](https://trustedrouter.com/docs/agent-setup) |
| `ant-ling` | `https://api.ant-ling.com/v1` (Chat Completions); Anthropic `https://api.ant-ling.com/anthropic` | manual (no documented Models API) | [OpenAI compatible](https://developer.ant-ling.com/en/docs/api-reference/openai/), [Anthropic compatible](https://developer.ant-ling.com/en/docs/api-reference/anthropic/), [Claude Code](https://developer.ant-ling.com/en/docs/integrations/claude-code/) |
| `antdigital-dtmaas` | `https://maas-api.antdigital.com/v1` (Chat Completions default, Responses) | manual (no documented Models API) | [Platform introduction](https://maas.antdigital.com/dt-maas-docs/intro), [Quick access](https://maas.antdigital.com/dt-maas-docs/quick-access), [Codex guide](https://maas.antdigital.com/dt-maas-docs/agent-guides/codex), [API Key](https://maas.antdigital.com/dt-maas-docs/console/api-key) |
| `unisound` | `https://maas-api.unisound.com/v1` (Chat Completions default, Responses); Anthropic `https://maas-api.unisound.com/anthropic` | `GET /v1/models`, credential required | [Quickstart](https://maas.unisound.com/docs/guide/quickstart), [OpenAI compatible](https://maas.unisound.com/docs/api/text/openai-compatible), [Anthropic compatible](https://maas.unisound.com/docs/api/text/anthropic-compatible), [Models API](https://maas.unisound.com/docs/api/models/openai-compatible), [Codex](https://maas.unisound.com/docs/toolAI/codex) |

Decisions:

- Default protocol is Responses only when the vendor documents it for the whole catalog. IteraCompute states its free Qwen model is not established on Responses, so Chat Completions stays the default there. DTMaaS and Unisound document Responses through their Codex guides but lead with Chat Completions.
- Clean APIs' coding-agent guide sets `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=https://cleanapis.com/v1`, but `/v1/messages` and `/v1/v1/messages` both return 404, so no Anthropic endpoint is exposed. Its marketing (flat-rate access to frontier proprietary models) suggests a reseller; users remain responsible for the vendor's terms.
- Munito's EU (`api.eu.munito.ai`) and US (`api.us.munito.ai`) hosts infer to the same template; users switch region by editing the Base URL.
- DTMaaS pay-as-you-go keys and Token Plan for Agent keys share the same endpoint and differ only by key type, so one template covers both. Unisound Token Plan keys likewise share its endpoint.
- Ant Ling answers `GET /v1/models` with an auth error, but the route is undocumented; DTMaaS returns the same auth error for any path. Both are excluded from discovery, and DTMaaS requires the exact model Code shown in its console.
- Portals: key pages are used where the official docs or offer name them (Clean APIs, IteraCompute, LLMTR, Munito, PayPerQ, River, TrustedRouter `/console` → `/console/api-keys`); otherwise the account console is used (Dolphin wallet app, Nous Portal, Ant Ling API Console, DTMaaS, Unisound).

Not added from the same list: Batchrate (batch-only; direct `POST /v1/chat/completions` returns `unknown_route` per its docs), HAL-X THX-01, Nace Console (Drex) and SubQ Snap (decision APIs on `/v1/systemone`; SubQ's chat model is early access only), ScaleDown (fixed summarize/extract/classify task models, no streaming), Empero (key-less community endpoint, offline for maintenance, no documented Base URL), Perch AI (agent apps, no public API), plus agents/IDEs, media tools, local aggregators and directories.

## FLock API Platform — 2026-10-13

`flock` is a gateway Provider for FLock.io's unified, OpenAI-compatible model API. The [API endpoint guide](https://docs.flock.io/flock-products/api-platform/api-endpoint) documents `POST https://api.flock.io/v1/chat/completions`, giving the MUX Base URL `https://api.flock.io/v1` with the Chat Completions protocol. No Responses or Anthropic Messages endpoint is documented, so none is exposed.

The official example authenticates with `x-litellm-api-key: sk-...` because the platform is LiteLLM-backed. MUX sends the standard `Authorization: Bearer` header; an invalid Bearer token sent to `https://api.flock.io/v1/models` and `/v1/chat/completions` returned LiteLLM's `token_not_found_in_db` error naming the received key, confirming the proxy reads Bearer credentials.

Model discovery uses the authenticated `GET /v1/models` with the OpenAI catalog decoder and the required credential policy; an unauthenticated request returned HTTP 401 (`No api key passed in`). The public marketing catalog at <https://platform.flock.io/api/models> uses a different schema and is listed at <https://platform.flock.io/models>; it is not used for discovery. Accounts and keys are created through the [getting started guide](https://docs.flock.io/flock-products/api-platform/getting-started) and the [platform console](https://platform.flock.io/), which is the template portal.

## GMI Cloud — 2026-10-09

`gmi-cloud` is a separate gateway Provider with Chat Completions at `https://api.gmi-serving.com/v1/chat/completions`, Responses at `https://api.gmi-serving.com/v1/responses`, and Anthropic Messages at `https://api.gmi-serving.com/v1/messages`. Combining the documented protocol bases gives the normalized Arca Base URL `https://api.gmi-serving.com` and paths `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/responses`, and `/v1/messages`.

Official sources: [LLM API](https://docs.gmicloud.ai/inference-engine/api-reference/llm-api-reference), [Codex integration](https://docs.gmicloud.ai/coding-tools/codex), [Claude Code integration](https://docs.gmicloud.ai/coding-tools/claude-code), and [API key guide](https://docs.gmicloud.ai/cluster-engine/user-management/api-keys). The guide links `https://console.gmicloud.ai/user-setting/api-keys`; its unauthenticated redirect preserves the key-page destination behind sign-in.

Model discovery uses the documented authenticated `GET /v1/models`, with the existing OpenAI catalog decoder and required Bearer credential policy. An unauthenticated request returned HTTP 401 (`API key is required`). Users supply their own credential source; no account, key, or saved connection is created by adding this template.

[Qwen's announcement](https://x.com/Alibaba_Qwen/status/2108401307577090259) advertises temporary access to Qwen3.8 Max/Flash and Wan3.0. The published text catalog does not yet establish the two new chat model IDs. Load exact IDs from the account's live catalog or enter a verified ID manually; do not hard-code guessed spellings or permanent free pricing. Wan3.0 uses the separate video API and is not an Agent chat profile. GMI's router API is also separate from these protocol endpoints.

## Official credential portals — 2026-10-04

`data/provider-links.json` is the default link catalog for all 214 built-in Provider templates (215 templates including Custom). Each entry retains `docs_url` and adds a typed `portal` containing a credential-free HTTPS URL and one of `api-key`, `console`, or `setup`. Core validates coverage, known Provider IDs, URL schemes, and absence of embedded credentials before serializing it. Custom and unknown connections have no default vendor link; no website is inferred from the user's Base URL.

The desktop's selected Provider banner, picker selection footer, and editor form use a core-provided portal: **Get API Key**, **Open console**, or **Setup guide**. Key-page entries include API Tokens and Access Tokens; account/region/subscription consoles are explicitly labeled as consoles, not promised as direct key pages. Ollama, LM Studio, and vLLM are local connection templates and retain setup guides rather than unrelated cloud account keys. The editor shows the effective address next to a browser-open action and can restore the template default. URLs open with the native system-browser opener without selecting a template, submitting a form, generating a key, or modifying a saved connection. Opener failures use the existing toast UI.

Each saved Provider connection can persist an optional `portal_url` override. Core accepts only a credential-free HTTPS URL without a fragment and resolves the effective portal from the override or template. CLI Provider list/show and desktop use that same resolved value; templates retain their defaults. `arca model provider docs` and `docs_url` keep their documentation semantics. The portal URL is not exported to Agent configuration, used for model discovery, or treated as an inference endpoint. API credentials remain in Keychain.

Portal sources are the official key pages and linked quickstarts reviewed on this date. Login redirects and JavaScript-only shells confirm an account entry, not successful authenticated key creation. Stable console entries are used when an authenticated resource/team/plan selection prevents verification of a direct key route. Temporary OAuth URLs, guessed team IDs, referral codes, and credentials are never recorded.

Notable evidence and mapping decisions:

| Provider family | Official reference / observed entry | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| OpenRouter | [Account key page](https://openrouter.ai/workspaces/default/keys) | Use the current workspace key route; all models of a Provider use the same account entry. |
| Mistral | [API key guide](https://docs.mistral.ai/admin/identity-access/api-keys) | Follow the guide's `admin.mistral.ai/plateforme/api-keys` link rather than a model documentation page. |
| Anthropic | [Claude Platform keys](https://platform.claude.com/settings/keys) | Use the current Claude Platform account domain. |
| Moonshot | [China keys](https://platform.moonshot.cn/console/api-keys), [Global keys](https://platform.moonshot.ai/console/api-keys) | Store the observed `platform.kimi.com` / `platform.kimi.ai` destinations separately. Kimi Code keeps its membership console. |
| Alibaba | [Coding Plan](https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/coding-plan), [Token Plan CN](https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/token-plan-personal-quick-start), [Token Plan Global](https://www.alibabacloud.com/help/en/model-studio/token-plan-team-quickstart) | PAYG keys, Coding Plan, and Token Plan have distinct portals; retain China and international account separation. |
| Xiaomi MiMo | [API integration FAQ](https://mimo.mi.com/docs/zh-CN/quick-start/faq/api-integration) | Official links enter the platform console. PAYG uses API Keys; regional Token Plans use the subscription's key, not the PAYG key. |
| MiniMax | [Global access](https://platform.minimax.io/console/access), [Token Plan](https://platform.minimax.io/docs/token-plan/intro) | PAYG access and plan console remain distinct; preserve CN/Global account domains. |
| StepFun | [Global plan quickstart](https://platform.stepfun.ai/docs/en/step-plan/quick-start), [China keys](https://platform.stepfun.com/interface-key) | The official quickstart links to `interface-key`; preserve each region. |
| Tencent | [Coding Plan FAQ](https://cloud.tencent.com/document/product/1823/130103), [Global Token Plan](https://intl.cloud.tencent.com/document/product/1300/81315) | Use regional TokenHub consoles; Coding and Token Plan keys are separate subscription credentials. |
| Baidu | [Plan entry](https://cloud.baidu.com/product/codingplan) | The official plan link opens `qianfan/resource/token-plan`; ordinary API keys use IAM. |
| DigitalOcean | [Manage model access keys](https://docs.digitalocean.com/products/inference/how-to/manage-model-access-keys/) | Follow the official `model-studio/manage-keys` link, not an Agent endpoint key. |
| Weights & Biases | [Official SDK](https://github.com/wandb/wandb) | The documented account settings entry redirects to `forge.coreweave.com/wandb/settings`. |
| Token Harbor | [Official CLI connection guide](https://tokenharbor.ai/docs/connect) | Add the formerly missing template entry; the guide identifies `/dashboard/api-keys`. |
| Routeway / Infron / APInex | [Routeway dashboard](https://routeway.ai/dashboard), [Infron quickstart](https://infron.ai/docs), [APInex platform](https://apinex.bond/) | Infron documents `/dashboard/apiKeys`; Routeway and APInex use official account consoles where a deeper key route could not be verified. |

## Provider expansion — 2026-10-04

Added 30 reviewed templates: Portkey, Helicone AI Gateway, PPIO, Infini AI GenStudio, AIHubMix, 302.AI, OpenCode Zen/Go, Kilo Gateway, NanoGPT, Synthetic, Chutes, Featherless AI, Venice, FriendliAI, Inference.net, OVHcloud AI Endpoints, Upstage, AI21 Labs, ZenMux, BytePlus ModelArk, Tencent TokenHub China/Global, LongCat, iFLYTEK Spark X2, Nscale, io.net, Inception, Ollama Cloud, and the official Tinfoil local proxy. The [research and endpoint matrix](../../../../docs/provider-gap-research-2026-10-04.md) records protocol references, public catalog observations, manual discovery boundaries, and excluded or retired candidates. Seventeen templates enable reviewed model discovery; the remaining thirteen require a model ID or an explicit catalog URL. No stored user connection or credential is rewritten.

## Documentation references — 2026-09-21

URLs were checked against the official pages on this date, using the existing source matrices and models.dev for discovery only. Regions and Coding/Token Plans retain their specific documentation. Direct requests to some vendors encountered network-policy or anti-bot pages; official page retrieval separately confirmed MiniMax CN, Kimi Code, Requesty, Together AI, Scaleway and StepFun Global. Network interception URLs are not stored in the catalog. On 2026-11-10 the MiMo Token Plan site route `/tokenplan/Token%20Plan/quick-access` served a product-release title; the verified guide is `https://mimo.mi.com/static/docs/tokenplan/Token%20Plan/quick-access.md`.

Plan-specific Provider templates were audited on 2026-07-28 against
`https://models.dev/api.json` for discovery and the vendors' official
documentation for authority. A Coding/Token Plan is a separate connection:
its API key and endpoint must not be mixed with the vendor's pay-as-you-go API.

Many plan products are restricted to interactive coding tools and supported
agents. Arca only stores the user-selected connection and credentials locally;
users remain responsible for the vendor's plan eligibility and usage policy.

The China MiniMax template endpoints were reverified on 2026-09-07 against the current official Token Plan and OpenAI/Anthropic SDK guides (`api.minimax.cn`). Existing saved connections are not rewritten.

The September 2026 expansion adds 19 templates and removes the retired GitHub Models new-connection template. See the [full endpoint/source matrix](../../../../docs/agent-provider-research-2026-09-07.md). Shared MiniMax PAYG/plan addresses do not identify billing mode; explicit plan selections are preserved.

## Gateway Provider templates

These gateway templates were reviewed on 2026-09-20. They are selectable
first-class Arca Providers, not aliases for the underlying model vendors. Arca
keeps the OpenAI Responses/Completions and Anthropic Messages paths as separate
protocol entries so one Provider can be reused by Agents with different
protocol requirements.

APInex is currently exposed with Chat Completions and Anthropic Messages only;
its public documentation does not establish a separate Responses endpoint.
Model IDs remain dynamic and are loaded from each Provider's `/models` catalog
when available rather than being hard-coded from social media posts.
## Native protocol additions — 2026-10-26

Google AI Studio supports [Interactions](https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/interactions-overview)
at `POST /v1beta/interactions` (`model`, `input`, `x-goog-api-key`). New connections
prefer this protocol while retaining generateContent. Arca's cURL examples opt
out of stored interaction state with `store=false`; the Models catalog remains
the Google `GET /v1beta/models` endpoint. Selecting `@ai-sdk/google` alone does
not select the separate `google.interactions(...)` factory, so Arca's current
Agent writers must reject Interactions assignment rather than emit generateContent.
This boundary was checked against OpenCode's current stable
[v1.19.4 provider loader](https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/blob/v1.19.4/packages/opencode/src/provider/provider.ts),
which calls `sdk.languageModel(...)`. Its dev-branch v2 native Interactions
provider is not a supported stable-client contract.

Amazon Bedrock [Converse](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/APIReference/API_runtime_Converse.html)
uses the regional `https://bedrock-runtime.<region>.amazonaws.com` endpoint and
`POST /model/{model}/converse`. Its content-block `messages` body has no `model`
field. The [official API-key workflow](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/api-keys-use.html)
supports Bearer authentication. Arca reuses its current Keychain/environment/file/helper
credential sources for this key without adding IAM credential administration.
The [Amazon Bedrock AI SDK provider](https://ai-sdk.dev/providers/ai-sdk-providers/amazon-bedrock)
supports `baseURL` and Bearer `apiKey`, which are mapped by the OpenCode/Kilo writers.
Converse model discovery is manual; it must not probe a fabricated `/models` route.
